# Tasks (iteration 19) Implementation Plan


**Goal:** Daily stage tasks, recurring machine upkeep and long-term tasks (from Suggest machines' **Plan this**) per room, a room **Tasks** tab, a farm **Today** page, worker names and task rules in Settings, and a log with CSV export, as specified in `docs/specs/2026-10-30-tasks-design.md`.

**Architecture:** Pure rule logic in `server/task-rules.js` decides the tasks a room wants on a local day; `server/tasks.js` makes the rows lazily and idempotently (`ensureDay`) in a new `tasks` table (database 19) and closes them; `server/tasks-api.js` serves them; the web gets a Tasks card on the room page, a Today page, two Settings cards and a Plan this dialog on Suggest machines. Live updates through the existing SSE bus.

**Tech Stack:** Node 22 (ES modules, `node:sqlite`-style db module already in `server/db.js`), plain browser JS modules (no build), `node --test` (`npm test`).

---

## Ground rules (every part)

- Branch `feat/tasks` in worktree `.claude/worktrees/tasks` off `v2` (58694cd or later). Never touch `data/` of the main checkout; tests use `openDb(':memory:')`.
- TDD: failing test first, run it, implement, run it, commit. `npm test` must stay green at every commit (1771 tests at the start).
- Commit identity: this repo uses Ray Tham's GitHub noreply (already the repo's git config). No attribution trailers.
- Metric units, plain English words in the UI, sentence case, the app's existing tokens/classes (see `DESIGN.md`).
- Local day = Asia/Kuala_Lumpur, always through `localDate()` below (never the host's TZ).

## File structure

| File | New/changed | Responsibility |
|---|---|---|
| `server/task-rules.js` | new | pure: families, built-in daily and upkeep rules, crop notes, `localDate`, `addDays`, `stageDayOn`, `effectiveRules`, `wantedDaily`, `upkeepApplies` |
| `server/db.js` | changed | version 19: tables `tasks`, `workers`, `task_rule_settings`, `custom_task_rules`; task methods; `deleteRoom` deletes tasks |
| `server/tasks.js` | new | `createTasks({ db, now })`: `ensureDay`, `ensureAll`, `roomView`, `todayView`, `done`, `skip`, `cancel`, `addLongterm`, `addFromSuggestion`, `dueCount` |
| `server/tasks-api.js` | new | HTTP routes of spec section 6 (incl. CSV), workers and task-rules routes |
| `server/api.js` | changed | mount tasks routes, publish `room {id, part:'tasks'}` and `tasks {due}` |
| `server/controller.js` | changed | once per room per local day: `tasks.ensureDay(room.id, date)` (errors logged, never stop the cycle) |
| `server/main.js` | changed | create `tasks` and pass it to api and controller |
| `web/tasks-math.js` | new | pure: grouping by slot, overdue words, row line texts, long-term line, Setup update values from a machine change, suggestion → machine changes, CSV-free |

| `web/today-page.js` | new | `#/today` page |
| `web/task-dialogs.js` | new | Done (amount), Skip/Cancel (reason), worker picker, long-term Done with Setup update |
| `web/route.js`, `web/room-detail.js`, `web/app.js`, `web/index.html`, `web/styles.css` | changed | sixth tab `tasks` between crop and simulations, `#/today` route, top bar Today link with count |
| `web/settings-page.js` (+ `web/settings-math.js`) | changed | Workers card, Task rules card |
| `web/suggest-page.js` | changed | **Plan this** button and dialog |
| `README.md` | changed | a Tasks section |

## Shared contract (all parts use exactly these names and shapes)

### `server/task-rules.js` exports

```js
export const FAMILY_OF = { oyster: 'oyster', black: 'oyster', pink: 'oyster', abalone: 'oyster', golden: 'oyster',
  woodear: 'woodear', reishi: 'reishi', milky: 'milky', lionsmane: 'lionsmane' };
export const SLOTS = ['morning', 'afternoon', 'any'];
export const DAILY_RULES;   // [{ key, kind:'daily', text, families: string[]|null, stages: string[]|null, when, slot, amount: { unit, label }|null }]
                            // when: 'daily' | 'first' | 'last' | { every: N, lastDays: M|null }   (lastDays null = whole stage)
export const CLEAR_OUT;     // the done program's rule { key:'clearOut', kind:'daily', text, when:'daily', slot:'morning', amount:null }
export const UPKEEP_RULES;  // [{ key, kind:'upkeep', text, everyDays, needs: 'ac'|'humidifier'|'dehumidifier'|'co2'|'trh'|null }]
export const CROP_NOTES;    // { [cropKey]: { harvest?: string, [ruleKey]?: string } }  harvest applies to harvestAm/harvestPm/harvestDaily/reishiRim
export function localDate(ms) → 'YYYY-MM-DD'               // Asia/Kuala_Lumpur
export function addDays(date, n) → 'YYYY-MM-DD'
export function daysBetween(a, b) → integer                  // b − a in days
export function stageDayOn(program, date) → { day, length } | null   // day 1 = the local date of program.stageSince; null when no stageSince
export function effectiveRules({ settings, custom, roomId }) → { daily: Rule[], upkeep: Rule[] }
        // settings: [{ key, enabled, everyDays }]; custom: [{ id, text, rooms: number[]|null, everyDays: number|null, slot, enabled }]
        // custom rule keys are `custom:<id>`; custom with everyDays null → daily rule (families null, stages null, when 'daily')
export function wantedDaily({ program, crops, date, rules }) → [{ ruleKey, text, note, slot, unit }]
        // rules = effectiveRules(...).daily; program.done → only CLEAR_OUT (+ footbath, floorClean, custom daily); no crops → footbath, floorClean, custom daily
export function upkeepApplies(rule, room) → boolean   // needs: 'ac' → always true; 'humidifier' → true; 'dehumidifier' → room.dehumidifier;
                                                      // 'co2' → room.co2Address !== null; 'trh' → room.trhAddress !== null; null → true
```

(Check the real room field names for the CO₂ and T/RH sensor addresses in `server/db.js` rowToRoom and use those.)

### Task row (db → API → web), camelCase

```js
{ id, roomId, kind: 'daily'|'upkeep'|'longterm', ruleKey: string|null, date: 'YYYY-MM-DD'|null, slot: 'morning'|'afternoon'|'any',
  text, note: string|null, status: 'open'|'done'|'skipped'|'missed'|'cancelled', dueAt: ms|null, createdAt: ms, closedAt: ms|null,
  worker: string|null, amount: number|null, unit: string|null, reason: string|null,
  source: { machine, from, to, rmMonth, suggestedAt }|null,
  amountLabel: string|null }   // amountLabel/unit filled from the rule for open rows that record an amount
```

`source.machine` ∈ `'fanFlow'|'acBTU'|'humidifierLH'|'dehumidifierLday'` (the sizing keys); `from`/`to` numbers; `rmMonth` = the suggestion's RM a month minus the baseline's (negative = cheaper).

### `server/tasks.js`: `createTasks({ db, now = Date.now })` returns

```js
ensureDay(roomId, date = localDate(now()))      // spec section 4; returns nothing; idempotent
ensureAll(date)                                 // ensureDay for every room
roomView(roomId) → { date, today: Row[], upkeep: Row[], longterm: Row[], counts: { due, overdue, longterm } }
        // today: daily rows of date (all statuses); upkeep: open upkeep rows sorted by dueAt, each + overdueDays (>=0 integer, 0 = due today or not due) and dueDate;
        // longterm: open longterm rows; counts.due = open daily rows of date + open upkeep rows with dueDate <= date
todayView() → { date, rooms: [{ id, name, today: Row[], upkeepDue: Row[], longterm: number, counts }] }   // Overview room order (db.listRooms order)
done(id, { worker, amount }) → Row      // throws HttpError-like { status, message, field }: 404, 409 'Already done by Ali at 09:12', 400 worker/amount
skip(id, { worker, reason }) → Row      // not longterm (400); upkeep: next row due addDays(date of skip, everyDays)
cancel(id, { reason }) → Row            // longterm only (400 otherwise)
addLongterm(roomId, { text, note }) → Row
addFromSuggestion(roomId, { machines: [{ key, from, to }], rmMonth, suggestedAt }) → Row[]   // one row per machine where from !== to; none → 400 'Nothing changes from what the room has now'
log(roomId, { before = null, limit = 50 }) → Row[]   // closed rows, newest closedAt first, id < before
logCsv(roomId) → string                              // header: date,time,room,task,status,worker,amount,unit,reason
dueCount() → number                                  // sum of every room's counts.due for today
onChange(fn)                                         // fn(roomId) after any change (made rows, closed rows)
```

Errors thrown are `HttpError` from `server/api.js` if it is exported there, else a local `TaskError extends Error { status, field }` that `tasks-api.js` turns into `{ error, field }` like the other modules.

Long-term text from a suggestion (made by the server): `Fan 150 → 200 m³/h`, `Aircon 9,000 → 12,000 BTU/h`, `Humidifier 0.5 → 1 L/h`, `Dehumidifier none → 10 L/day` (0 = none), numbers grouped with `,`.

### HTTP (spec section 6, exact)

`GET /api/rooms/:id/tasks` → roomView · `GET /api/tasks/today` → todayView · `POST /api/tasks/:id/done {worker, amount?}` · `POST /api/tasks/:id/skip {worker, reason}` · `POST /api/tasks/:id/cancel {reason}` · `POST /api/rooms/:id/tasks {text, note?}` or `{suggestion:{machines, rmMonth, suggestedAt}}` → `{ rows: Row[] }` · `GET /api/rooms/:id/tasks/log?before=` → `{ rows }` · `GET /api/rooms/:id/tasks/log.csv` (text/csv, `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="<room name> tasks.csv"`) · `GET/PUT /api/workers` → `{ workers: [{ id, name, active }] }` · `GET/PUT /api/task-rules` → `{ builtin: [{ key, kind, text, families, stages, when, slot, everyDays, enabled }], custom: [{ id, text, rooms, everyDays, slot, enabled }] }`.

SSE: after any change `publish('room', { id, part: 'tasks' })` and `publish('tasks', { due: tasks.dueCount() })`; the web listens with `live.on('room')` (part 'tasks') and `live.on('tasks')`.

### Web

- `ROOM_TABS` gains `{ key: 'tasks', label: 'Tasks' }` between crop and simulations; `#/rooms/:id/tasks`.
- `#/today` route → `web/today-page.js` `mount(view)` returning an unmount function (same shape as other pages in `web/app.js`).
- Worker kept per browser: `localStorage['farm.worker']` (a name string).
- Top bar: a **Today** link (`href="#/today"`) between the alarm button and Overview, with a count badge from `GET /api/tasks/today`'s total and the `tasks` SSE event.

## Lead reconciliation (read before executing)

Parts were written in parallel against the contract above and then checked against each other. Execute in order **A → B → C**.

| Point | Decision |
|---|---|
| db method names | part A's: `taskRuleSettings`, `setTaskRuleSettings`, `customTaskRules`, `setCustomTaskRules`, `listWorkers` (by name), `setWorkers` (part B updated to them) |
| Errors | `server/tasks.js` exports `TaskError(status, message, field)`; `tasks-api.js` turns any 4xx `status` into `{ error, field }` |
| Skipping a long-term task | 400 "A long-term task is done or cancelled, not skipped" (both parts) |
| Swapping two workers' names | `setWorkers` parks kept names first (`replaceList`'s `before`), tested in A5 |
| SSE | published once, by `api.js` from `tasks.onChange` (B7); `main.js` only makes the one `tasks` and hands it to the controller and `createApp` (A11). B7's `main.js` edits are then already present: check and skip |
| `PUT /api/task-rules` | `{ builtin?: [{ key, enabled?, everyDays? }], custom?: [...] }`: a given `builtin` entry changes only its key; a given `custom` replaces the list (part C sends both) |
| `PUT /api/workers` | `{ workers: [...] }` whole list; part C sends a removed worker as `active: false` |
| `web/live.js` `EVENT_TYPES` | gains `tasks` (C4) |
| Length limits | reason ≤ 200, own task text 1–120, note ≤ 500, worker name ≤ 40 (server and client agree) |
| Room not found | `roomView`/`logCsv` return null; routes check the room first and reply 404 "Room not found" |

Part A was replayed on a copy of v2 58694cd (1806 tests passing); part C on another copy (1812 passing, screenshots of the pages against mocked routes). Part B was checked against part A by reading, not run: expect small fixes while executing it.

```js
// The task rules (server/task-rules.js, spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md sections 3 and 4): the farm's day, the stage day,
// the daily rules a room wants on a day, the owner's changes and the upkeep conditions.

import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { localDate, localTime, dayStart, addDays, daysBetween, stageDayOn } from '../server/task-rules.js';
import { PROGRAM_DEFAULTS } from '../server/program.js';

const at = (iso) => Date.parse(iso);
const START = at('2026-10-30T09:15:00+08:00'); // a stage started on 30 Oct, farm time
const prog = (extra = {}) => ({ ...PROGRAM_DEFAULTS, farmType: 'full', crops: [{ crop: 'oyster', blocks: 100 }], stageSince: START, ...extra });

test('the farm day is Kuala Lumpur time whatever the host clock', () => {
  assert.equal(localDate(at('2026-10-29T16:00:00Z')), '2026-10-30'); // 00:00 farm time
  assert.equal(localDate(at('2026-10-29T15:59:59Z')), '2026-10-29');
  assert.equal(localTime(at('2026-10-30T01:12:00Z')), '09:12');
  assert.equal(dayStart('2026-10-30'), at('2026-10-30T00:00:00+08:00'));
  assert.equal(addDays('2026-10-30', 3), '2026-11-02');
  assert.equal(addDays('2026-03-01', -1), '2026-02-28');
  assert.equal(daysBetween('2026-10-30', '2026-11-02'), 3);
  assert.equal(daysBetween('2026-11-02', '2026-10-30'), -3);
});

test('stageDayOn: day 1 is the farm date of the stage start, not capped; null without a start', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(stageDayOn(prog({ stage: 'fruiting' }), '2026-10-30'), { day: 1, length: 7 });
  assert.deepEqual(stageDayOn(prog({ stage: 'fruiting' }), '2026-11-05'), { day: 7, length: 7 });
  assert.deepEqual(stageDayOn(prog({ stage: 'fruiting' }), '2026-11-08'), { day: 10, length: 7 });
  // 23:30 farm time is still that day
  assert.equal(stageDayOn(prog({ stageSince: at('2026-10-30T23:30:00+08:00') }), '2026-10-31').day, 2);
  assert.equal(stageDayOn(prog({ stageSince: null }), '2026-10-30'), null);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the code**

Create `server/task-rules.js`:

```js
// The task rules (spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md section 3): the built-in daily stage rules and upkeep rules, the crop
// notes, the farm's local day, and which tasks a room wants on a day. Pure: no database, no clock.
//
// A daily rule is { key, kind: 'daily', text, families: string[] | null, stages: string[] | null, when, slot, amount: { unit, label } | null };
// when is 'daily', 'first' (day 1 of the stage), 'last' (the stage's last day by days[stage]) or { every: N, lastDays: M | null }
// (every N days within the stage's last M days, counted from day 1 of that window; lastDays null: the whole stage).
// An upkeep rule is { key, kind: 'upkeep', text, everyDays, needs } (needs: what the room must have, null for nothing).

const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
// The farm's day is Asia/Kuala_Lumpur's: UTC+8 all year (Malaysia has had no daylight saving since 1982), so a fixed offset
// gives the same dates as the time zone, without depending on the host's TZ or its ICU data.
const OFFSET_MS = 8 * 3_600_000;

const midnightUtc = (date) => Date.parse(`${date}T00:00:00Z`);

// 'YYYY-MM-DD' of `ms` on the farm's clock.
export const localDate = (ms) => new Date(ms + OFFSET_MS).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

// 'HH:MM' of `ms` on the farm's clock.
export const localTime = (ms) => new Date(ms + OFFSET_MS).toISOString().slice(11, 16);

// The ms when the farm's day `date` starts.
export const dayStart = (date) => midnightUtc(date) - OFFSET_MS;

export const addDays = (date, n) => new Date(midnightUtc(date) + n * DAY_MS).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

// b − a in whole days.
export const daysBetween = (a, b) => Math.round((midnightUtc(b) - midnightUtc(a)) / DAY_MS);

// The day of the program's stage on `date` (day 1 = the farm's date of stageSince; not capped at the length) and the stage's
// length (days[stage]). Null without a start.
export function stageDayOn(program, date) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(program?.stageSince)) return null;
  return { day: daysBetween(localDate(program.stageSince), date) + 1, length: program.days?.[program.stage] ?? null };
}
```

```bash
git commit -m "Task rules, the farm's day (tasks, iteration 19): localDate, localTime and dayStart on Asia/Kuala_Lumpur time (UTC+8 all year, whatever the host's TZ), addDays, daysBetween, and stageDayOn (day 1 = the farm date of the stage start, not capped at the length)"
```

```js
// The task rules (server/task-rules.js, spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md sections 3 and 4): the farm's day, the stage day,
// the daily rules a room wants on a day, the owner's changes and the upkeep conditions.

import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {
  FAMILY_OF, SLOTS, DAILY_RULES, CLEAR_OUT, UPKEEP_RULES, CROP_NOTES, localDate, localTime, dayStart, addDays, daysBetween,
  stageDayOn, effectiveRules, wantedDaily, upkeepApplies,

import { CROP_KEYS } from '../web/crops.js';

const START = at('2026-10-30T09:15:00+08:00'); // a stage started on 30 Oct, farm time
const prog = (extra = {}) => ({ ...PROGRAM_DEFAULTS, farmType: 'full', crops: [{ crop: 'oyster', blocks: 100 }], stageSince: START, ...extra });
const ALL = effectiveRules({ roomId: 1 }).daily;
```

and append at the end of the file:

```js

test('the built-in rules are the spec tables', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(DAILY_RULES.map((r) => r.key), ['contamWalk', 'colonisedCheck', 'openBags', 'casingApply', 'reishiForm', 'humidifierCheck',
    'harvestAm', 'harvestPm', 'harvestDaily', 'reishiRim', 'scrapeRecap', 'reopen', 'footbath', 'floorClean']);
  for (const r of [...DAILY_RULES, CLEAR_OUT]) assert.ok(SLOTS.includes(r.slot), r.key);
  assert.deepEqual(DAILY_RULES.filter((r) => r.amount).map((r) => [r.key, r.amount.unit]),
    [['contamWalk', 'bags'], ['harvestAm', 'kg'], ['harvestPm', 'kg'], ['harvestDaily', 'kg'], ['reishiRim', 'kg']]);
  assert.deepEqual(UPKEEP_RULES.map((r) => [r.key, r.everyDays, r.needs]), [
    ['acFilter', 7, 'ac'], ['humidifierDescale', 7, 'humidifier'], ['flyTraps', 7, null], ['dehumidifierFilter', 14, 'dehumidifier'],
    ['co2Check', 30, 'co2'], ['rhCheck', 90, 'trh'], ['acService', 120, 'ac'], ['acChemWash', 180, 'ac']]);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(FAMILY_OF).sort(), [...CROP_KEYS].sort(), 'every crop has a family');
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(CROP_NOTES).sort(), ['abalone', 'black', 'golden', 'lionsmane', 'pink', 'woodear']);
});

test('an oyster room through its stages', () => {
  const spawn = prog({ stage: 'spawn' }); // 21 days: 30 Oct to 19 Nov
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-10-30', spawn), ['contamWalk', 'footbath', 'floorClean']);
  const pinning = prog({ stage: 'pinning' });
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-10-30', pinning), ['contamWalk', 'openBags', 'humidifierCheck', 'footbath', 'floorClean']);
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-10-31', pinning), ['contamWalk', 'humidifierCheck', 'footbath', 'floorClean']);
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-10-31', prog({ stage: 'fruiting' })),
    ['contamWalk', 'humidifierCheck', 'harvestAm', 'harvestPm', 'footbath', 'floorClean']);
  const rest = prog({ stage: 'rest' }); // 10 days: 30 Oct to 8 Nov
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-10-30', rest), ['contamWalk', 'scrapeRecap', 'footbath', 'floorClean'], 'first day');
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-11-02', rest), ['contamWalk', 'footbath', 'floorClean']);
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-11-08', rest), ['contamWalk', 'reopen', 'footbath', 'floorClean'], 'last day');
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-11-09', rest), ['contamWalk', 'footbath', 'floorClean'], 'after the last day');
});

test('the colonisation check comes every 2 days within the last 7 days of the spawn run', () => {
  const spawn = prog({ stage: 'spawn' }); // day 15 = 13 Nov, day 21 = 19 Nov
  const days = [];
  for (let d = 1; d <= 22; d++) if (keysOn(addDays('2026-10-30', d - 1), spawn).includes('colonisedCheck')) days.push(d);
  assert.deepEqual(days, [15, 17, 19, 21]);
  assert.ok(!keysOn('2026-11-13', prog({ stage: 'spawn', stageSince: null })).includes('colonisedCheck'), 'no start: no stage day');
});

test('the families: wood ear, milky, reishi and lion\'s mane', () => {
  const crop = (c, extra) => prog({ crops: [{ crop: c, blocks: 10 }], ...extra });
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-10-30', crop('milky', { stage: 'pinning' })), ['contamWalk', 'casingApply', 'humidifierCheck', 'footbath', 'floorClean']);
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-10-30', crop('reishi', { stage: 'pinning' })),
    ['contamWalk', 'openBags', 'reishiForm', 'humidifierCheck', 'footbath', 'floorClean']);
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-10-30', crop('woodear', { stage: 'fruiting' })), ['contamWalk', 'humidifierCheck', 'harvestDaily', 'footbath', 'floorClean']);
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-10-30', crop('lionsmane', { stage: 'fruiting' })),
    ['contamWalk', 'humidifierCheck', 'harvestAm', 'harvestPm', 'footbath', 'floorClean']);
  const reishi = crop('reishi', { stage: 'fruiting' });
  assert.ok(keysOn('2026-10-30', reishi).includes('reishiRim'), 'day 1');
  assert.ok(!keysOn('2026-10-31', reishi).includes('reishiRim'), 'day 2');
  assert.ok(keysOn('2026-11-01', reishi).includes('reishiRim'), 'day 3');
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-11-08', crop('woodear', { stage: 'rest' })), ['contamWalk', 'reopen', 'footbath', 'floorClean']);
  assert.ok(!keysOn('2026-11-08', crop('milky', { stage: 'rest' })).includes('reopen'), 'milky does not reopen');
});

test('mixed crops: each rule once, with the notes of each crop that has one', () => {
  const p = prog({ stage: 'fruiting', crops: [{ crop: 'pink', blocks: 10 }, { crop: 'golden', blocks: 10 }, { crop: 'oyster', blocks: 5 }] });
  const wanted = wantedDaily({ program: p, crops: p.crops, date: '2026-10-30', rules: ALL });
  assert.equal(wanted.filter((w) => w.ruleKey === 'harvestAm').length, 1);
  const am = wanted.find((w) => w.ruleKey === 'harvestAm');
  assert.deepEqual(am, {
    ruleKey: 'harvestAm', text: 'Harvest check: pick before the cap edge waves or splits; pick before any misting',
    note: 'Pink oyster: Keeps 1–3 days after picking: pick on sale days · Golden oyster: Fragile when mature: pick early, handle gently',
    slot: 'morning', unit: 'kg',
  });
  assert.equal(wanted.find((w) => w.ruleKey === 'contamWalk').unit, 'bags');
  assert.equal(wanted.find((w) => w.ruleKey === 'footbath').note, null);
  const one = prog({ stage: 'fruiting', crops: [{ crop: 'lionsmane', blocks: 10 }] });
  const lm = wantedDaily({ program: one, crops: one.crops, date: '2026-10-30', rules: ALL });
  assert.equal(lm.find((w) => w.ruleKey === 'harvestPm').note, 'Spines 6–13 mm and still white; brown or yellow means now');
  assert.equal(lm.find((w) => w.ruleKey === 'humidifierCheck').note, 'Mist the air, never the mushroom');
  const ab = prog({ stage: 'rest', crops: [{ crop: 'abalone', blocks: 10 }, { crop: 'woodear', blocks: 10 }] });
  assert.equal(wantedDaily({ program: ab, crops: ab.crops, date: '2026-11-01', rules: ALL }).find((w) => w.ruleKey === 'contamWalk').note,
    'Abalone oyster: Black droplets on the mycelium are normal, not contamination');
});

test('a done program gives clearOut with the room rules; no crops gives the room rules only', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-10-30', prog({ stage: 'rest', done: true })), ['clearOut', 'footbath', 'floorClean']);
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-10-30', prog({ crops: [] })), ['footbath', 'floorClean']);
  assert.ok(!keysOn('2026-10-30', prog({ stage: 'fruiting' })).includes('clearOut'));
});

test('effectiveRules: rules off, upkeep intervals and the owner\'s rules per room', () => {
  const settings = [{ key: 'harvestPm', enabled: false, everyDays: null }, { key: 'acFilter', enabled: true, everyDays: 10 },
    { key: 'acChemWash', enabled: false, everyDays: null }];
  const custom = [
    { id: 1, text: 'Top up the RO water tank', rooms: null, everyDays: null, slot: 'afternoon', enabled: true },
    { id: 2, text: 'Clean the shelves', rooms: [2], everyDays: 14, slot: 'any', enabled: true },
    { id: 3, text: 'Old rule', rooms: null, everyDays: null, slot: 'any', enabled: false },
  ];
  const r1 = effectiveRules({ settings, custom, roomId: 1 });
  assert.ok(!r1.daily.some((r) => r.key === 'harvestPm'));
  assert.equal(r1.upkeep.find((r) => r.key === 'acFilter').everyDays, 10);
  assert.equal(r1.upkeep.find((r) => r.key === 'flyTraps').everyDays, 7);
  assert.ok(!r1.upkeep.some((r) => r.key === 'acChemWash'));
  assert.deepEqual(r1.daily.at(-1), { key: 'custom:1', kind: 'daily', text: 'Top up the RO water tank', families: null, stages: null, when: 'daily', slot: 'afternoon', amount: null });
  assert.ok(!r1.upkeep.some((r) => r.key === 'custom:2'), 'room 2 only');
  assert.ok(!r1.daily.some((r) => r.key === 'custom:3'), 'turned off');
  const r2 = effectiveRules({ settings, custom, roomId: 2 });
  assert.deepEqual(r2.upkeep.at(-1), { key: 'custom:2', kind: 'upkeep', text: 'Clean the shelves', everyDays: 14, needs: null, slot: 'any' });
  // a custom daily rule is made with or without crops
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-10-30', prog({ crops: [] }), r1.daily), ['footbath', 'floorClean', 'custom:1']);
  assert.ok(!keysOn('2026-10-30', prog({ stage: 'fruiting' }), r1.daily).includes('harvestPm'));
  const off = effectiveRules({ settings: [{ key: 'clearOut', enabled: false, everyDays: null }], roomId: 1 });
  assert.deepEqual(keysOn('2026-10-30', prog({ done: true }), off.daily), ['footbath', 'floorClean']);
});

test('upkeepApplies: what the room has', () => {
  const room = { dehumidifier: false, co2Address: null, trhAddress: 3 };
  const rule = (key) => UPKEEP_RULES.find((r) => r.key === key);
  assert.deepEqual(UPKEEP_RULES.filter((r) => upkeepApplies(r, room)).map((r) => r.key),
    ['acFilter', 'humidifierDescale', 'flyTraps', 'rhCheck', 'acService', 'acChemWash']);
  assert.equal(upkeepApplies(rule('dehumidifierFilter'), { ...room, dehumidifier: true }), true);
  assert.equal(upkeepApplies(rule('co2Check'), { ...room, co2Address: 2 }), true);
  assert.equal(upkeepApplies(rule('rhCheck'), { ...room, trhAddress: null }), false);
});
```

```js
};
const HARVEST_KEYS = new Set(['harvestAm', 'harvestPm', 'harvestDaily', 'reishiRim']);
// The crop's name before its note in a room of several crops.
const NOTE_NAMES = { pink: 'Pink oyster', golden: 'Golden oyster', black: 'Black oyster', abalone: 'Abalone oyster', lionsmane: "Lion's mane", woodear: 'Wood ear' };

// The rules of the room itself (made with or without crops, and while the program is done).
const isRoomRule = (r) => r.key === 'footbath' || r.key === 'floorClean' || r.key.startsWith('custom:');
```

Then append at the end of the file (after `stageDayOn`'s closing `}`, with one blank line):

```js
// The rules of one room after the owner's changes: built-in rules turned off are left out, upkeep intervals replaced, and the
// owner's own rules for this room added (key `custom:<id>`; everyDays null: a daily rule, else an upkeep rule).
// settings: [{ key, enabled, everyDays }]; custom: [{ id, text, rooms: number[] | null, everyDays, slot, enabled }].
export function effectiveRules({ settings = [], custom = [], roomId }) {
  const byKey = new Map(settings.map((s) => [s.key, s]));
  const on = (r) => byKey.get(r.key)?.enabled !== false;
  const daily = [CLEAR_OUT, ...DAILY_RULES].filter(on);
  const upkeep = UPKEEP_RULES.filter(on).map((r) => ({ ...r, everyDays: byKey.get(r.key)?.everyDays ?? r.everyDays }));
  for (const c of custom) {
    if (!c.enabled || (c.rooms !== null && !c.rooms.includes(roomId))) continue;
    const key = `custom:${c.id}`;
    if (c.everyDays === null) daily.push({ key, kind: 'daily', text: c.text, families: null, stages: null, when: 'daily', slot: c.slot, amount: null });
    else upkeep.push({ key, kind: 'upkeep', text: c.text, everyDays: c.everyDays, needs: null, slot: c.slot });
  }
  return { daily, upkeep };
}

function whenApplies(when, sd) {
  if (when === 'daily') return true;
  if (sd === null || sd.day < 1) return false;
  if (when === 'first') return sd.day === 1;
  if (when === 'last') return sd.length !== null && sd.day === sd.length;
  let start = 1;
  if (when.lastDays !== null) {
    if (sd.length === null) return false;
    start = Math.max(1, sd.length - when.lastDays + 1);
  }
  return sd.day >= start && (sd.day - start) % when.every === 0;
}

// The note of a rule for the room's crops: one crop's note as it is; several crops' notes each after the crop's name, joined
// with ' · '. Null without a note.
function noteOf(ruleKey, cropKeys) {
  const notes = [];
  for (const crop of cropKeys) {
    const n = CROP_NOTES[crop]?.[ruleKey] ?? (HARVEST_KEYS.has(ruleKey) ? CROP_NOTES[crop]?.harvest : undefined);
    if (n) notes.push({ crop, n });
  }
  if (notes.length === 0) return null;
  if (cropKeys.length === 1) return notes[0].n;
  return notes.map(({ crop, n }) => `${NOTE_NAMES[crop]}: ${n}`).join(' · ');
}

// The daily tasks a room wants on `date`: [{ ruleKey, text, note, slot, unit }] in the rules' order, each rule once (a room of
// several crops gets the notes of each). rules: effectiveRules(...).daily. No crops: the room's own rules only (footbath,
// floorClean, custom); a done program: clearOut and those.
export function wantedDaily({ program, crops = program?.crops ?? [], date, rules }) {
  const cropKeys = [...new Set(crops.map((c) => c.crop))];
  const families = new Set(cropKeys.map((k) => FAMILY_OF[k]).filter(Boolean));
  let picked;
  if (cropKeys.length === 0) picked = rules.filter(isRoomRule);
  else if (program.done) picked = rules.filter((r) => r.key === CLEAR_OUT.key || isRoomRule(r));
  else {
    const sd = stageDayOn(program, date);
    picked = rules.filter((r) => isRoomRule(r) || (r.key !== CLEAR_OUT.key
      && (r.stages === null || r.stages.includes(program.stage))
      && (r.families === null || r.families.some((f) => families.has(f)))
      && whenApplies(r.when, sd)));
  }
  return picked.map((r) => ({ ruleKey: r.key, text: r.text, note: noteOf(r.key, cropKeys), slot: r.slot, unit: r.amount?.unit ?? null }));
}

// Whether an upkeep rule applies to a room (a db room: dehumidifier, co2Address, trhAddress). The aircon and the humidifier
// count as always there (every room has them).
export function upkeepApplies(rule, room) {
  switch (rule.needs) {
    case 'dehumidifier': return room.dehumidifier === true;
    case 'co2': return room.co2Address !== null;
    case 'trh': return room.trhAddress !== null;
    default: return true; // 'ac', 'humidifier', null
  }
}
```

Notes for the reviewer (decided here, see the summary at the end):
- `CLEAR_OUT` is part of `effectiveRules().daily` (first, so it leads a done room's list) and can be turned off with the setting key `clearOut`; `wantedDaily` uses it only when `program.done`.
- `contamWalk` is "all families" but needs crops: a room with no crops gets only `footbath`, `floorClean` and the owner's daily rules.
- With no `stageSince` only `when: 'daily'` rules apply (no stage day is known).
- A custom upkeep rule carries its `slot` (the built-in upkeep rules have none: `'any'`).
- Crop notes: one crop, the note as it is; several crops, `"<crop>: <note>"` joined with ` · `. The spec's lower-case starts after a rule key (`contamWalk: black droplets …`) are capitalised as the note stands alone.

```js
// Tasks (spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md 5): the rooms' daily, upkeep and long-term tasks, the worker names, the owner's
// changes of the built-in rules and the owner's own rules. A daily row is unique per room, rule, date and slot; an upkeep
// rule has at most one open row per room.
const TASKS = `
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tasks (
  id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  room_id    INTEGER NOT NULL,
  kind       TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('daily', 'upkeep', 'longterm')),
  rule_key   TEXT,
  date       TEXT,
  slot       TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'any' CHECK (slot IN ('morning', 'afternoon', 'any')),
  text       TEXT    NOT NULL,
  note       TEXT,
  status     TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' CHECK (status IN ('open', 'done', 'skipped', 'missed', 'cancelled')),
  due_at     INTEGER,
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  closed_at  INTEGER,
  worker     TEXT,
  amount     REAL,
  unit       TEXT,
  reason     TEXT,
  source     TEXT
) STRICT;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS tasks_daily ON tasks (room_id, rule_key, date, slot) WHERE kind = 'daily';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS tasks_upkeep_open ON tasks (room_id, rule_key) WHERE kind = 'upkeep' AND status = 'open';
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS tasks_by_status ON tasks (room_id, status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS tasks_closed ON tasks (room_id, closed_at, id) WHERE status <> 'open';
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS workers (
  id     INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  name   TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,
  active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (active IN (0, 1))
) STRICT;
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS task_rule_settings (
  key        TEXT    PRIMARY KEY,
  enabled    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (enabled IN (0, 1)),
  every_days INTEGER CHECK (every_days BETWEEN 1 AND 365)
) STRICT;
-- AUTOINCREMENT: a removed rule's id (its rows' rule key custom:<id>) is never given to a new rule
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS custom_task_rules (
  id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  text       TEXT    NOT NULL,
  rooms      TEXT,
  every_days INTEGER CHECK (every_days BETWEEN 1 AND 365),
  slot       TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'any' CHECK (slot IN ('morning', 'afternoon', 'any')),
  enabled    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (enabled IN (0, 1))
) STRICT;`;

```

```js
    // ---- Tasks (spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md 4 and 5). A task is taskRow's shape. ----

    // A daily row, unless the room already has one of that rule, date and slot (any status). → true when it was made.
    insertDailyTask({ roomId, ruleKey, date, slot, text, note = null, unit = null, at }) {
      return q.insertDailyTask.run(roomId, ruleKey, date, slot, text, note, unit, at).changes > 0;
    },

    // An open upkeep row due at dueAt, unless the rule already has an open row in the room. → true when it was made.
    insertUpkeepTask({ roomId, ruleKey, slot = 'any', text, dueAt, at }) {
      return q.insertUpkeepTask.run(roomId, ruleKey, slot, text, dueAt, at).changes > 0;
    },

    // A long-term row (source: a suggestion's { machine, from, to, rmMonth, suggestedAt } or null). → the row.
    insertLongtermTask({ roomId, text, note = null, source = null, at }) {
      const { lastInsertRowid } = q.insertLongtermTask.run(roomId, text, note, source === null ? null : JSON.stringify(source), at);
      return taskRow(q.task.get(Number(lastInsertRowid)));
    },

    getTask(id) {
      const r = q.task.get(id);
      return r ? taskRow(r) : null;
    },

    // The room's daily rows of a date (every status), oldest first.
    dailyTasks(roomId, date) {
      return q.dailyTasks.all(roomId, date).map(taskRow);
    },

    // The room's open rows of a kind: upkeep by due time, the others oldest first.
    openTasks(roomId, kind) {
      return q.openTasks.all(roomId, kind).map(taskRow);
    },

    // The room's open daily rows of dates before `date`.
    openDailyBefore(roomId, date) {
      return q.openDailyBefore.all(roomId, date).map(taskRow);
    },

    // The open row of an upkeep rule in the room, or null.
    openUpkeep(roomId, ruleKey) {
      const r = q.openUpkeep.get(roomId, ruleKey);
      return r ? taskRow(r) : null;
    },

    // The last done or skipped row of an upkeep rule in the room, or null.
    lastClosedUpkeep(roomId, ruleKey) {
      const r = q.lastClosedUpkeep.get(roomId, ruleKey);
      return r ? taskRow(r) : null;
    },

    // Closes an open row: { status, at, worker, amount, reason }; next: an upkeep row to make in the same transaction
    // (insertUpkeepTask's fields). → false when the row is not open (nothing changes then).
    closeTask(id, { status, at, worker = null, amount = null, reason = null }, next = null) {
      db.exec('BEGIN');
      try {
        const closed = q.closeTask.run(status, at, worker, amount, reason, id).changes > 0;
        if (closed && next) this.insertUpkeepTask(next);
        db.exec('COMMIT');
        return closed;
      } catch (err) {
        db.exec('ROLLBACK');
        throw err;
      }
    },

    // The room's closed rows, newest closedAt first, `limit` of them, older than the row `before` (an id) when given.
    taskLog(roomId, { before = null, limit = 50 } = {}) {
      return q.taskLog.all(roomId, before, limit).map(taskRow);
    },

    // Every closed row of the room, oldest closedAt first (the CSV).
    taskLogAll(roomId) {
      return q.taskLogAll.all(roomId).map(taskRow);
    },
```

Insert after `checkName` (`  function checkName(name, id) { … }`, lines 835–837) and its blank line, before `  // Each address may be used by one device …`:

```js
  // Replaces the rows of a list table in one transaction: rows whose id is not in the list are deleted, list items with a
  // stored id updated, the others inserted. → read() after it.
  // before(kept): runs after the deletes, before the updates (workers park their names there, so a swap of two names never
  // trips UNIQUE(name) halfway).
  function replaceList(all, remove, list, update, insert, read, before = null) {
    db.exec('BEGIN');
    try {
      const stored = new Set(all.all().map((r) => r.id));
      const kept = new Set(list.filter((x) => stored.has(x.id)).map((x) => x.id));
      for (const id of stored) if (!kept.has(id)) remove.run(id);
      before?.(kept);
      for (const x of list) {
        if (kept.has(x.id)) update(x);
        else insert(x);
      }
      db.exec('COMMIT');
    } catch (err) {
      db.exec('ROLLBACK');
      throw err;
    }
    return read();
  }
```

Insert before `    close() {` (after the task row methods of A4):

```js
    // ---- Workers and task rules (spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md 3.4 and 7.3). The lists must be valid (tasks-api.js). ----

    // [{ id, name, active }] by name.
    listWorkers() {
      return q.workers.all().map((r) => ({ id: r.id, name: r.name, active: r.active === 1 }));
    },

    // Replaces the list: a worker with a known id is updated (a rename), one without is added, one left out is removed (the
    // tasks keep the name as text). → the new list.
    setWorkers(list) {
      return replaceList(q.workers, q.deleteWorker, list, (w) => q.updateWorker.run(w.name, w.active ? 1 : 0, w.id),
        (w) => q.insertWorker.run(w.name, w.active ? 1 : 0), () => this.listWorkers(),
        (kept) => { for (const id of kept) q.parkWorker.run(`\u0000${id}`, id); });
    },

    // The owner's changes of the built-in rules: [{ key, enabled, everyDays }] (everyDays null: the rule's own).
    taskRuleSettings() {
      return q.ruleSettings.all().map((r) => ({ key: r.key, enabled: r.enabled === 1, everyDays: r.every_days }));
    },

    // Replaces every change of the built-in rules. → the new list.
    setTaskRuleSettings(list) {
      db.exec('BEGIN');
      try {
        q.clearRuleSettings.run();
        for (const s of list) q.insertRuleSetting.run(s.key, s.enabled ? 1 : 0, s.everyDays ?? null);
        db.exec('COMMIT');
      } catch (err) {
        db.exec('ROLLBACK');
        throw err;
      }
      return this.taskRuleSettings();
    },

    // The owner's own rules: [{ id, text, rooms: number[] | null, everyDays: number | null, slot, enabled }] by id.
    customTaskRules() {
      return q.customRules.all().map((r) => ({
        id: r.id, text: r.text, rooms: r.rooms === null ? null : JSON.parse(r.rooms), everyDays: r.every_days, slot: r.slot, enabled: r.enabled === 1,
      }));
    },

    // Replaces the owner's rules like setWorkers (a rule left out is removed; its rows stay). → the new list.
    setCustomTaskRules(list) {
      const values = (c) => [c.text, c.rooms == null ? null : JSON.stringify(c.rooms), c.everyDays ?? null, c.slot, c.enabled ? 1 : 0];
      return replaceList(q.customRules, q.deleteCustomRule, list, (c) => q.updateCustomRule.run(...values(c), c.id),
        (c) => q.insertCustomRule.run(...values(c)), () => this.customTaskRules());
    },
```

The lists must already be valid: `tasks-api.js` (part B) checks names (not empty, not taken ignoring case, within the list too), intervals 1–365, rule texts 1–120 characters, slots and room ids before it calls these. A name taken twice reaching the database throws SQLite's `UNIQUE constraint failed`.

```js
// The tasks module (server/tasks.js, spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md sections 2, 4 and 6): making a day's rows, missed and
// cancelled rows, done, skip and cancel with their errors, upkeep intervals, long-term rows, the views, the log and the CSV.

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { openDb } from '../server/db.js';

const at = (iso) => Date.parse(iso);
const DAY = 86_400_000;
const ROOM = { name: 'Room 1', length: 6, width: 4, height: 2.5, trhAddress: 1 };
const OYSTER = [{ crop: 'oyster', blocks: 100 }];

function setup(t, { crops = OYSTER, stage = 'fruiting', room = {} } = {}) {
  const db = openDb(':memory:');
  t.after(() => db.close());
  let clock = at('2026-10-30T08:00:00+08:00');
  const tasks = createTasks({ db, now: () => clock, log: (e) => { throw e; } });
  const id = db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, ...room }, clock).id;
  if (crops.length) db.setProgram(id, { ...db.getProgram(id), crops, stage, stageSince: clock });
  const changes = [];
  tasks.onChange((roomId) => changes.push(roomId));
  return {
    db, tasks, id, changes,
    set: (iso) => { clock = at(iso); },
    later: (ms) => { clock += ms; },
    keys: (rows) => rows.map((r) => r.ruleKey),
    row: (key) => tasks.roomView(id).today.find((r) => r.ruleKey === key),
  };
}

// Calls fn and checks the TaskError it throws.
function refused(fn, status, message, field) {
  assert.throws(fn, (err) => {
    assert.ok(err instanceof TaskError);
    assert.deepEqual([err.status, err.message, err.field], [status, message, field]);
    return true;
  });
}

test('ensureDay makes the day\'s daily rows and the upkeep rows once', (t) => {
  const s = setup(t);
  s.tasks.ensureDay(s.id, '2026-10-30');
  s.tasks.ensureDay(s.id, '2026-10-30');
  assert.deepEqual(s.changes, [s.id], 'one change: the second call made nothing');
  const v = s.tasks.roomView(s.id);
  assert.equal(v.date, '2026-10-30');
  assert.deepEqual(s.keys(v.today), ['contamWalk', 'humidifierCheck', 'harvestAm', 'harvestPm', 'footbath', 'floorClean']);
  assert.deepEqual(v.today.map((r) => r.slot), ['morning', 'morning', 'morning', 'afternoon', 'morning', 'afternoon']);
  // no dehumidifier and no CO₂ sensor: their rules are left out
  assert.deepEqual(s.keys(v.upkeep), ['acFilter', 'humidifierDescale', 'flyTraps', 'rhCheck', 'acService', 'acChemWash']);
  assert.deepEqual(v.upkeep.map((r) => [r.dueDate, r.overdueDays]), Array(6).fill(['2026-10-30', 0]));
  assert.deepEqual(v.counts, { due: 12, overdue: 0, longterm: 0 });
  const harvest = v.today.find((r) => r.ruleKey === 'harvestAm');
  assert.deepEqual([harvest.unit, harvest.amountLabel, harvest.status, harvest.kind], ['kg', 'kg picked', 'open', 'daily']);
  assert.equal(v.today.find((r) => r.ruleKey === 'footbath').amountLabel, null);
});

test('the machines a room has give their upkeep rules', (t) => {
  const s = setup(t, { room: { co2Address: 2, dehumidifier: true } });
  assert.deepEqual(s.keys(s.tasks.roomView(s.id).upkeep),
    ['acFilter', 'humidifierDescale', 'flyTraps', 'dehumidifierFilter', 'co2Check', 'rhCheck', 'acService', 'acChemWash']);
});

test('open daily rows are missed after their day; closed ones stay as they are', (t) => {
  const s = setup(t);
  s.tasks.ensureDay(s.id);
  s.db.closeTask(s.row('footbath').id, { status: 'done', at: at('2026-10-30T08:30:00+08:00'), worker: 'Ali' });
  s.set('2026-10-31T07:00:00+08:00');
  const v = s.tasks.roomView(s.id);
  assert.equal(v.date, '2026-10-31');
  const old = s.db.dailyTasks(s.id, '2026-10-30');
  assert.deepEqual(old.map((r) => [r.ruleKey, r.status]), [['contamWalk', 'missed'], ['humidifierCheck', 'missed'], ['harvestAm', 'missed'],
    ['harvestPm', 'missed'], ['footbath', 'done'], ['floorClean', 'missed']]);
  assert.equal(old[0].closedAt, at('2026-10-31T00:00:00+08:00'), 'missed when the day ended');
  assert.ok(v.today.every((r) => r.status === 'open'));
  assert.equal(v.upkeep.length, 6, 'upkeep rows are never missed');
  assert.equal(v.upkeep[0].overdueDays, 1);
});

test('a stage change cancels today\'s rows that no longer apply; a rule turned off keeps its row', (t) => {
  const s = setup(t);
  s.tasks.ensureDay(s.id);
  s.db.setTaskRuleSettings([{ key: 'contamWalk', enabled: false }]);
  s.later(3 * 3_600_000);
  s.db.setProgram(s.id, startStage(s.db.getProgram(s.id), 'rest', at('2026-10-30T11:00:00+08:00'), s.db.getControl(s.id)));
  const today = s.tasks.roomView(s.id).today;
  const status = Object.fromEntries(today.map((r) => [r.ruleKey, r.status]));
  assert.deepEqual(status, { contamWalk: 'open', humidifierCheck: 'cancelled', harvestAm: 'cancelled', harvestPm: 'cancelled',
    footbath: 'open', floorClean: 'open', scrapeRecap: 'open' });
  assert.equal(today.find((r) => r.ruleKey === 'harvestAm').reason, 'Stage changed');
});

test('the owner\'s rules: a daily rule for some rooms, an upkeep rule for all', (t) => {
  const s = setup(t, { crops: [] });
  const other = s.db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, name: 'Room 2', trhAddress: null }).id;
  s.db.setCustomTaskRules([
    { text: 'Top up the RO water tank', rooms: [s.id], everyDays: null, slot: 'afternoon', enabled: true },
    { text: 'Clean the shelves', rooms: null, everyDays: 14, slot: 'any', enabled: true },
  ]);
  const [daily, upkeep] = s.db.customTaskRules();
  const mine = s.tasks.roomView(s.id);
  assert.deepEqual(s.keys(mine.today), ['footbath', 'floorClean', `custom:${daily.id}`], 'no crops: the room rules and the own daily rule');
  assert.deepEqual(mine.today.at(-1).text, 'Top up the RO water tank');
  assert.equal(mine.upkeep.at(-1).ruleKey, `custom:${upkeep.id}`);
  const theirs = s.tasks.roomView(other);
  assert.deepEqual(s.keys(theirs.today), ['footbath', 'floorClean']);
  assert.ok(theirs.upkeep.some((r) => r.ruleKey === `custom:${upkeep.id}`));
});

test('a done program gives the clear-out task', (t) => {
  const s = setup(t, { stage: 'rest' });
  s.db.setProgram(s.id, { ...s.db.getProgram(s.id), done: true });
  assert.deepEqual(s.keys(s.tasks.roomView(s.id).today), ['clearOut', 'footbath', 'floorClean']);
});

test('ensureAll makes every room\'s day; a missing room has no view', (t) => {
  const s = setup(t);
  const other = s.db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, name: 'Room 2', trhAddress: null }).id;
  s.tasks.ensureAll('2026-10-30');
  assert.deepEqual(s.changes.sort(), [s.id, other].sort());
  assert.equal(s.tasks.roomView(999), null);
  s.tasks.ensureDay(999); // no such room: nothing, no error
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the code**

Create `server/tasks.js`:

```js
// The rooms' tasks (spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md sections 2, 4 and 6): makes a day's rows when they are first needed
// (ensureDay: idempotent), closes rows (done, skip, cancel), adds long-term rows (the owner's, or Plan this on Suggest
// machines), and reads the views of the Tasks tab, the Today page and the log.

import {
  DAILY_RULES, localDate, localTime, dayStart, addDays, daysBetween, effectiveRules, wantedDaily, upkeepApplies,
} from './task-rules.js';

// A refused request: status 400, 404 or 409, field the input it is about (tasks-api.js replies { error, field }).
export class TaskError extends Error {
  constructor(status, message, field = undefined) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'TaskError';
    this.status = status;
    this.field = field;
  }
}

const AMOUNT_LABEL = Object.fromEntries(DAILY_RULES.filter((r) => r.amount).map((r) => [r.key, r.amount.label]));

export function createTasks({ db, now = Date.now, log = console.error }) {
  const listeners = new Set();
  const today = () => localDate(now());

  function tell(roomId) {
    for (const fn of [...listeners]) {
      try {
        fn(roomId);
      } catch (err) {
        log(err);
      }
    }
  }

  const rulesFor = (roomId) => effectiveRules({ settings: db.taskRuleSettings(), custom: db.customTaskRules(), roomId });

  // A row as the API shows it: amountLabel for an open row whose rule records an amount.
  const view = (row) => ({ ...row, amountLabel: row.status === 'open' ? AMOUNT_LABEL[row.ruleKey] ?? null : null });

  // An open upkeep row with its due date and how many days it is overdue on `date` (0: due today or not yet due).
  const upkeepView = (row, date) => {
    const dueDate = localDate(row.dueAt);
    return { ...view(row), dueDate, overdueDays: Math.max(0, daysBetween(dueDate, date)) };
  };

  // Spec section 4: the day's wanted daily rows made, open daily rows of earlier days missed, today's open rows whose rule
  // is no longer wanted cancelled ('Stage changed'; a rule turned off keeps its row), and each applying upkeep rule's open row
  // made when it has none (due everyDays after its last done or skip, or today without one).
  function ensureDay(roomId, date = today()) {
    const room = db.getRoom(roomId);
    if (!room) return;
    const at = now();
    const rules = rulesFor(roomId);
    const program = db.getProgram(roomId);
    const wanted = wantedDaily({ program, crops: program.crops, date, rules: rules.daily });
    let changed = false;
    for (const row of db.openDailyBefore(roomId, date)) {
      changed = db.closeTask(row.id, { status: 'missed', at: Math.min(at, dayStart(addDays(row.date, 1))) }) || changed;
    }
    const want = new Set(wanted.map((w) => `${w.ruleKey}/${w.slot}`));
    const on = new Set(rules.daily.map((r) => r.key));
    for (const row of db.dailyTasks(roomId, date)) {
      if (row.status !== 'open' || want.has(`${row.ruleKey}/${row.slot}`) || !on.has(row.ruleKey)) continue;
      changed = db.closeTask(row.id, { status: 'cancelled', at, reason: 'Stage changed' }) || changed;
    }
    for (const w of wanted) changed = db.insertDailyTask({ roomId, date, ...w, at }) || changed;
    for (const rule of rules.upkeep) {
      if (!upkeepApplies(rule, room) || db.openUpkeep(roomId, rule.key)) continue;
      const last = db.lastClosedUpkeep(roomId, rule.key);
      const due = last ? addDays(localDate(last.closedAt), rule.everyDays) : date;
      changed = db.insertUpkeepTask({ roomId, ruleKey: rule.key, slot: rule.slot ?? 'any', text: rule.text, dueAt: dayStart(due), at }) || changed;
    }
    if (changed) tell(roomId);
  }

  // The views of one room on `date` (made already).
  function viewOf(roomId, date) {
    const todayRows = db.dailyTasks(roomId, date).map(view);
    const upkeep = db.openTasks(roomId, 'upkeep').map((r) => upkeepView(r, date));
    const longterm = db.openTasks(roomId, 'longterm').map(view);
    const due = todayRows.filter((r) => r.status === 'open').length + upkeep.filter((r) => r.dueDate <= date).length;
    return { date, today: todayRows, upkeep, longterm, counts: { due, overdue: upkeep.filter((r) => r.overdueDays > 0).length, longterm: longterm.length } };
  }

  return {
    ensureDay,

    // ensureDay for every room.
    ensureAll(date = today()) {
      for (const room of db.listRooms()) ensureDay(room.id, date);
    },

    // The Tasks tab: { date, today, upkeep, longterm, counts: { due, overdue, longterm } }, or null when there is no such room.
    roomView(roomId) {
      if (!db.getRoom(roomId)) return null;
      const date = today();
      ensureDay(roomId, date);
      return viewOf(roomId, date);
    },

    // fn(roomId) after any change of a room's rows; → a function that stops it.
    onChange(fn) {
      listeners.add(fn);
      return () => listeners.delete(fn);
    },
  };
}
```

Notes: a missed row's `closedAt` is the start of the next farm day (the moment its day ended), or now when that is earlier. A day's open row whose rule was turned off stays open (spec 4: "its open rows stay until done or the day ends"); a row whose rule is on but no longer wanted (stage change, done program) is cancelled with the reason `Stage changed`. An upkeep rule without an open row is due `everyDays` after its last done or skip, or on `date` when it has none (a rule turned on again picks up its history).

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the code**

In `server/tasks.js`, insert above line 21 `export function createTasks({ db, now = Date.now, log = console.error }) {`:

```js
const MAX_AMOUNT = 10_000;
const MAX_REASON = 200;
const STATUS_WORD = { done: 'done', skipped: 'skipped', missed: 'missed', cancelled: 'cancelled' };

// '12,000', '0.5': thousands grouped with a comma.
const grouped = (n) => {
  const [whole, frac] = String(n).split('.');
  return `${whole.replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+$)/g, ',')}${frac ? `.${frac}` : ''}`;
};

// The text of a refused close: 'Already done by Ali at 09:12' (the farm's clock).
function closedText(row) {
  if (row.status === 'missed') return 'Already missed: its day has ended';
  const by = row.worker ? ` by ${row.worker}` : '';
  return `Already ${STATUS_WORD[row.status]}${by} at ${localTime(row.closedAt)}`;
}

const trimmed = (v) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v.trim() : '');
```

Insert above `  return {` (line 104 after the insert above; the line after `viewOf`'s closing brace and its blank line):

```js
  // The next row of an upkeep rule after one is closed on `date`, or null when the rule no longer applies (turned off,
  // removed, or the room no longer has the machine).
  function nextUpkeep(row, date, at) {
    const rule = rulesFor(row.roomId).upkeep.find((r) => r.key === row.ruleKey);
    const room = db.getRoom(row.roomId);
    if (!rule || !room || !upkeepApplies(rule, room)) return null;
    return { roomId: row.roomId, ruleKey: rule.key, slot: rule.slot ?? 'any', text: rule.text, dueAt: dayStart(addDays(date, rule.everyDays)), at };
  }

  // The open row `id` or a TaskError (404, 409).
  function openRow(id) {
    const row = db.getTask(id);
    if (!row) throw new TaskError(404, 'Task not found');
    if (row.status !== 'open') throw new TaskError(409, closedText(row));
    return row;
  }

  function workerOf(worker) {
    const name = trimmed(worker);
    if (!name) throw new TaskError(400, 'Pick who did it', 'worker');
    return name;
  }

  function reasonOf(reason) {
    const text = trimmed(reason);
    if (!text) throw new TaskError(400, 'Say why', 'reason');
    if (text.length > MAX_REASON) throw new TaskError(400, `The reason must be at most ${MAX_REASON} characters`, 'reason');
    return text;
  }

  // Closes the open row; a row closed by someone else meanwhile is a 409 too. An upkeep row makes its next row with it.
  function close(row, fields) {
    const at = now();
    const next = row.kind === 'upkeep' ? nextUpkeep(row, localDate(at), at) : null;
    if (!db.closeTask(row.id, { ...fields, at }, next)) throw new TaskError(409, closedText(db.getTask(row.id)));
    tell(row.roomId);
    return view(db.getTask(row.id));
  }
```

```js
    // { worker, amount }: amount only on a row that records one (empty: none), from 0 to 10,000.
    done(id, { worker, amount } = {}) {

          throw new TaskError(400, `The amount must be a number from 0 to ${grouped(MAX_AMOUNT)}`, 'amount');
        }
        value = amount;
      }
      return close(row, { status: 'done', worker: name, amount: value });
    },

    // { worker, reason }; not for a long-term row (that is cancelled).
    skip(id, { worker, reason } = {}) {
      const row = openRow(id);
      if (row.kind === 'longterm') throw new TaskError(400, 'A long-term task is done or cancelled, not skipped');
      return close(row, { status: 'skipped', worker: workerOf(worker), reason: reasonOf(reason) });
    },

    // { reason }; long-term rows only.
    cancel(id, { reason } = {}) {
      const row = openRow(id);
      if (row.kind !== 'longterm') throw new TaskError(400, 'Only a long-term task can be cancelled');
      return close(row, { status: 'cancelled', reason: reasonOf(reason) });
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the code**

In `server/tasks.js`, insert above line 40 `export function createTasks(…) {`:

```js
const MAX_TEXT = 120;
const MAX_NOTE = 500;
// The machines of a suggestion (the sizing keys): the name and unit of the long-term text; zero: the word for 0.
const MACHINES = {
  fanFlow: { name: 'Fan', unit: 'm³/h' },
  acBTU: { name: 'Aircon', unit: 'BTU/h' },
  humidifierLH: { name: 'Humidifier', unit: 'L/h' },
  dehumidifierLday: { name: 'Dehumidifier', unit: 'L/day', zero: 'none' },
};

// 'Aircon 9,000 → 12,000 BTU/h', 'Dehumidifier none → 10 L/day', 'Dehumidifier 10 L/day → none'.
export function machineChangeText(key, from, to) {
  const m = MACHINES[key];
  const word = (n) => (n === 0 && m.zero ? m.zero : grouped(n));
  return word(to) === m.zero ? `${m.name} ${word(from)} ${m.unit} → ${m.zero}` : `${m.name} ${word(from)} → ${word(to)} ${m.unit}`;
}
```

Insert above `    // fn(roomId) after any change of a room's rows; → a function that stops it.`:

```js
    // The owner's long-term task: { text (1–120 characters), note? (up to 500) }. → the row.
    addLongterm(roomId, { text, note } = {}) {
      if (!db.getRoom(roomId)) throw new TaskError(404, 'Room not found');
      const t = trimmed(text);
      if (!t || t.length > MAX_TEXT) throw new TaskError(400, `Say what to do (1 to ${MAX_TEXT} characters)`, 'text');
      const n = trimmed(note);
      if (n.length > MAX_NOTE) throw new TaskError(400, `The note must be at most ${MAX_NOTE} characters`, 'note');
      const row = db.insertLongtermTask({ roomId, text: t, note: n || null, at: now() });
      tell(roomId);
      return view(row);
    },

    // Plan this on Suggest machines: { machines: [{ key, from, to }], rmMonth, suggestedAt } → one long-term row per machine
    // that changes.
    addFromSuggestion(roomId, suggestion) {
      if (!db.getRoom(roomId)) throw new TaskError(404, 'Room not found');
      const { machines, rmMonth = null, suggestedAt = null } = suggestion ?? {};
      const number = (v) => typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v);
      if (!Array.isArray(machines) || machines.some((m) => !Object.hasOwn(MACHINES, m?.key) || !number(m.from) || !number(m.to) || m.from < 0 || m.to < 0)) {
        throw new TaskError(400, 'machines must be a list of { key, from, to } of the sized machines', 'suggestion');
      }
      if ((rmMonth !== null && !number(rmMonth)) || (suggestedAt !== null && !number(suggestedAt))) {
        throw new TaskError(400, 'rmMonth and suggestedAt must be numbers', 'suggestion');
      }
      const changes = machines.filter((m) => m.from !== m.to);
      if (changes.length === 0) throw new TaskError(400, 'Nothing changes from what the room has now', 'suggestion');
      const at = now();
      const rows = changes.map((m) => view(db.insertLongtermTask({
        roomId, text: machineChangeText(m.key, m.from, m.to),
        source: { machine: m.key, from: m.from, to: m.to, rmMonth, suggestedAt }, at,
      })));
      tell(roomId);
      return rows;
    },
```

In `server/tasks.js`, insert above line 57 `export function createTasks(…) {`:

```js
const LOG_PAGE = 50;
const CSV_HEADER = 'date,time,room,task,status,worker,amount,unit,reason';

// One CSV cell: quoted when it holds a comma, quote or line break; a text starting like a formula gets a ' first.
function cell(v) {
  if (v === null || v === undefined) return '';
  let s = String(v);
  if (typeof v === 'string' && /^[=+\-@]/.test(s)) s = `'${s}`;
  return /[",\r\n]/.test(s) ? `"${s.replaceAll('"', '""')}"` : s;
}
```

Insert above `    // fn(roomId) after any change of a room's rows; → a function that stops it.`:

```js
    // The Today page: every room in the Overview's order with its daily rows, its upkeep due today or overdue, how many
    // long-term rows are open, and its counts.
    todayView() {
      const date = today();
      const rooms = db.listRooms().map((room) => {
        ensureDay(room.id, date);
        const v = viewOf(room.id, date);
        return { id: room.id, name: room.name, today: v.today, upkeepDue: v.upkeep.filter((r) => r.dueDate <= date), longterm: v.counts.longterm, counts: v.counts };
      });
      return { date, rooms };
    },

    // The closed rows, newest first, LOG_PAGE at most, older than the row `before` when given.
    log(roomId, { before = null, limit = LOG_PAGE } = {}) {
      return db.taskLog(roomId, { before, limit }).map(view);
    },

    // Every closed row as CSV (oldest first): date, time (the farm's clock; none for a missed row), room, task, status, worker,
    // amount, unit, reason. Null when there is no such room.
    logCsv(roomId) {
      const room = db.getRoom(roomId);
      if (!room) return null;
      const lines = db.taskLogAll(roomId).map((r) => [
        r.date ?? localDate(r.closedAt), r.status === 'missed' ? '' : localTime(r.closedAt), room.name, r.text, r.status, r.worker,
        r.amount, r.amount === null ? null : r.unit, r.reason,
      ].map(cell).join(','));
      return `${[CSV_HEADER, ...lines].join('\r\n')}\r\n`;
    },

    // The sum of every room's counts.due today (the top bar's count), from the rows made so far.
    dueCount() {
      const date = today();
      return db.listRooms().reduce((sum, room) => sum + viewOf(room.id, date).counts.due, 0);
    },
```

`dueCount` reads the rows made so far and does not call `ensureDay`: it runs inside the `onChange` listener of A11, and making rows there would call the listeners again.

**Files:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/controller-tasks.test.js`:

```js
// The controller's cycle makes the rooms' tasks (spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md section 4): once per room per farm day, again
// after a stage change, an error logged without stopping the cycle.

import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createController } from '../server/controller.js';
import { openDb } from '../server/db.js';
import { createMachines } from '../server/machines.js';
import { createTasks } from '../server/tasks.js';

const at = (iso) => Date.parse(iso);
const DAY = 86_400_000;
const ROOM = { name: 'Room 1', length: 6, width: 4, height: 2.5 };

function setup(t, { tasks = null, failFor = null } = {}) {
  const db = openDb(':memory:');
  t.after(() => db.close());
  const client = { async writeCoil() {}, async writeRegisters() {} };
  const poller = { machineState: () => ({ relay: { address: null, polls: [] }, ac: { address: null, polls: [] } }) };
  let clock = at('2026-10-30T08:00:00+08:00');
  const now = () => clock;
  const calls = [];
  const fake = {
    ensureDay(roomId, date) {
      calls.push([roomId, date]);
      if (roomId === failFor) throw new Error('tasks broke');
    },
  };
  const errors = [];
  const machines = createMachines({ db, client, poller, now });
  const controller = createController({ db, machines, now, log: (e) => errors.push(e), tasks: tasks ? tasks({ db, now }) : fake });
  const id = db.createRoom(ROOM, clock).id;
  return {
    db, id, calls, errors,
    set: (iso) => { clock = at(iso); },
    at: () => clock,
    async cycle(dt = 30_000) {
      clock += dt;
      await controller.run(clock);
    },
  };
}

test('a room\'s tasks are made once per farm day', async (t) => {
  const s = setup(t);
  await s.cycle();
  await s.cycle();
  assert.deepEqual(s.calls, [[s.id, '2026-10-30']]);
  s.set('2026-10-30T23:59:00+08:00');
  await s.cycle();
  assert.equal(s.calls.length, 1, 'still 30 Oct on the farm clock');
  s.set('2026-10-31T00:00:00+08:00');
  await s.cycle();
  assert.deepEqual(s.calls.at(-1), [s.id, '2026-10-31']);
  assert.equal(s.calls.length, 2);
});

test('a stage change in the cycle makes the day again', async (t) => {
  const s = setup(t);
  s.db.setProgram(s.id, { ...s.db.getProgram(s.id), crops: [{ crop: 'oyster', blocks: 100 }], stage: 'fruiting', stageSince: s.at() - 7 * DAY + 60_000 });
  await s.cycle();
  assert.equal(s.calls.length, 1);
  await s.cycle(120_000); // fruiting ends: rest starts
  assert.equal(s.db.getProgram(s.id).stage, 'rest');
  assert.deepEqual(s.calls, [[s.id, '2026-10-30'], [s.id, '2026-10-30']]);
});

test('an error is logged once that day and the other rooms go on', async (t) => {
  const s = setup(t, { failFor: 1 });
  const other = s.db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, name: 'Room 2' }).id;
  await s.cycle();
  await s.cycle();
  assert.deepEqual(s.errors.map((e) => e.message), ['tasks broke']);
  assert.deepEqual(s.calls, [[s.id, '2026-10-30'], [other, '2026-10-30']]);
});

test('with the tasks module the cycle makes the rows', async (t) => {
  const s = setup(t, { tasks: createTasks });
  await s.cycle();
  assert.deepEqual(s.db.dailyTasks(s.id, '2026-10-30').map((r) => r.ruleKey), ['footbath', 'floorClean']);
  assert.deepEqual(s.errors, []);
});
```

## Interfaces delivered (part A)

### `server/task-rules.js`

| Export | Signature / value |
|---|---|
| `FAMILY_OF`, `SLOTS` | as the contract |
| `DAILY_RULES` | 14 rules of table 3.1; `amount`: `{ unit: 'bags', label: 'Bags removed' }` (contamWalk), `{ unit: 'kg', label: 'kg picked' }` (harvestAm, harvestPm, harvestDaily, reishiRim), else `null` |
| `CLEAR_OUT` | `{ key: 'clearOut', kind: 'daily', text, families: null, stages: null, when: 'daily', slot: 'morning', amount: null }` |
| `UPKEEP_RULES` | 8 rules of table 3.2: `{ key, kind: 'upkeep', text, everyDays, needs }` |
| `CROP_NOTES` | `{ pink, golden, black, abalone, lionsmane, woodear }` → `{ harvest?, contamWalk?, humidifierCheck? }` |
| `localDate(ms)` | `'YYYY-MM-DD'` on Asia/Kuala_Lumpur (UTC+8) |
| `localTime(ms)` | `'HH:MM'` on the farm clock (extra) |
| `dayStart(date)` | ms of the farm day's 00:00 (extra) |
| `addDays(date, n)`, `daysBetween(a, b)` | as the contract |
| `stageDayOn(program, date)` | `{ day, length } \| null`; day not capped; `length = program.days[program.stage]` |
| `effectiveRules({ settings = [], custom = [], roomId })` | `{ daily: [CLEAR_OUT?, …DAILY_RULES on, …custom daily], upkeep: [...on, everyDays replaced, …custom upkeep with slot] }` |
| `wantedDaily({ program, crops = program.crops, date, rules })` | `[{ ruleKey, text, note, slot, unit }]` |
| `upkeepApplies(rule, room)` | uses `room.dehumidifier`, `room.co2Address`, `room.trhAddress` |

### `server/db.js` (database 19)

| Method | Signature → result |
|---|---|
| `insertDailyTask` | `({ roomId, ruleKey, date, slot, text, note = null, unit = null, at })` → `boolean` (made) |
| `insertUpkeepTask` | `({ roomId, ruleKey, slot = 'any', text, dueAt, at })` → `boolean` |
| `insertLongtermTask` | `({ roomId, text, note = null, source = null, at })` → `Row` |
| `getTask` | `(id)` → `Row \| null` |
| `dailyTasks` | `(roomId, date)` → `Row[]` (every status, by id) |
| `openTasks` | `(roomId, kind)` → `Row[]` (open; by `due_at`, id) |
| `openDailyBefore` | `(roomId, date)` → `Row[]` |
| `openUpkeep` | `(roomId, ruleKey)` → `Row \| null` |
| `lastClosedUpkeep` | `(roomId, ruleKey)` → `Row \| null` (last done or skipped) |
| `closeTask` | `(id, { status, at, worker = null, amount = null, reason = null }, next = null)` → `boolean` (false: not open); `next` = `insertUpkeepTask` fields, same transaction |
| `taskLog` | `(roomId, { before = null, limit = 50 } = {})` → `Row[]` closed, newest `closedAt` first, older than row `before` |
| `taskLogAll` | `(roomId)` → `Row[]` closed, oldest first |
| `listWorkers` | `()` → `[{ id, name, active }]` by name |
| `setWorkers` | `([{ id?, name, active }])` → the new list (replace: update known ids, insert new, delete missing) |
| `taskRuleSettings` | `()` → `[{ key, enabled, everyDays }]` |
| `setTaskRuleSettings` | `([{ key, enabled, everyDays? }])` → the new list (replaces all) |
| `customTaskRules` | `()` → `[{ id, text, rooms: number[] \| null, everyDays: number \| null, slot, enabled }]` by id |
| `setCustomTaskRules` | `([{ id?, text, rooms, everyDays, slot, enabled }])` → the new list (replace like `setWorkers`; ids never reused) |
| `deleteRoom` | also deletes the room's tasks |

`Row` here is the contract's row without `amountLabel` (the tasks module adds it).

### `server/tasks.js`

| Export | Signature |
|---|---|
| `TaskError` | `class extends Error { status, field }`; `new TaskError(status, message, field?)` (`api.js` does not export `HttpError`) |
| `machineChangeText(key, from, to)` | `'Aircon 9,000 → 12,000 BTU/h'` (extra) |
| `createTasks({ db, now = Date.now, log = console.error })` | `ensureDay(roomId, date?)`, `ensureAll(date?)`, `roomView(roomId) → view \| null`, `todayView()`, `done(id, { worker, amount })`, `skip(id, { worker, reason })`, `cancel(id, { reason })`, `addLongterm(roomId, { text, note })`, `addFromSuggestion(roomId, { machines, rmMonth, suggestedAt })`, `log(roomId, { before, limit })`, `logCsv(roomId) → string \| null`, `dueCount()`, `onChange(fn) → unsubscribe` |

Errors (`TaskError`): 404 `Task not found` / `Room not found`; 409 `Already done by Ali at 09:12` · `Already skipped by Siti at 10:05` · `Already cancelled at 10:05` · `Already missed: its day has ended`; 400 `Pick who did it` (worker), `Say why` (reason), `The reason must be at most 200 characters` (reason), `The amount must be a number from 0 to 10,000` (amount), `A long-term task is done or cancelled, not skipped`, `Only a long-term task can be cancelled`, `Say what to do (1 to 120 characters)` (text), `The note must be at most 500 characters` (note), `Nothing changes from what the room has now` (suggestion), `machines must be a list of { key, from, to } of the sized machines` (suggestion), `rmMonth and suggestedAt must be numbers` (suggestion).

### `server/controller.js`, `server/main.js`

`createController({ …, tasks = null })`; `main.js` makes `tasks`, passes it to `createController` and `createApp(db, { …, tasks })`, (`api.js` publishes the events, B7).

# Part B: the tasks API, its mounting and the live events

Everything below runs in the worktree `.claude/worktrees/tasks` (branch `feat/tasks`), after part A is merged into it.
Paths are relative to the worktree root. Line numbers are those of `v2` 58694cd (`server/api.js` 315 lines, `server/app.js`
33 lines, `server/main.js`); find the quoted anchor text when part A has moved them.

## Needs from part A (the lead reconciles these with part A's plan)

| From | Name | What part B relies on |
|---|---|---|
| `server/db.js` | `listWorkers()` | `[{ id, name, active }]`, ordered by name (part A) |
| `server/db.js` | `setWorkers(list)` | `list: [{ id: number\|null, name, active }]` replaces the whole list: an id updates that worker, no id inserts, a stored id missing from the list is deleted. Must cope with swapped names (Ali↔Siti) without tripping `UNIQUE(name)` (e.g. delete-then-insert by id, or a two-phase rename). Return value unused. Names come trimmed, 1–40 characters, case-insensitively unique (checked by part B). |
| `server/db.js` | `taskRuleSettings()` | `[{ key, enabled: boolean, everyDays: number\|null }]` (null = the rule's default); keys without a row are on with the default |
| `server/db.js` | `setTaskRuleSettings(list)` | replaces every row with `list` (part B always sends one entry per built-in key, stored ones merged in) |
| `server/db.js` | `customTaskRules()` | `[{ id, text, rooms: number[]\|null, everyDays: number\|null, slot, enabled }]`, ordered by id |
| `server/db.js` | `setCustomTaskRules(list)` | same replace semantics as `setWorkers` (`id` null = new); return value unused |
| `server/task-rules.js` | `DAILY_RULES`, `CLEAR_OUT`, `UPKEEP_RULES`, `SLOTS`, `localDate`, `addDays` | as in the contract; daily rules carry `families`, `stages`, `when`, `slot` (null `families`/`stages` = all); upkeep rules `everyDays` and no `slot` (part B shows `'any'`) |
| `server/tasks.js` | `createTasks({ db, now })` | every contract method; `ensureAll(date)` takes a `'YYYY-MM-DD'` |
| `server/tasks.js` | errors | thrown errors carry `status` (400/404/409), `message`, optional `field` (part B turns any 4xx `status` into the API's `HttpError`; no import of `TaskError` needed). Exact texts part B's tests pin: 404 `Task not found`; 409 `Already done by Ali at 09:12` (KL time `HH:MM` of `closedAt`, same text for skip on a done row), cancelled rows `Already cancelled…`; skip of a long-term row 400 `A long-term task is done or cancelled, not skipped`; cancel of a daily/upkeep row 400 `Only a long-term task can be cancelled`; a suggestion with nothing changing 400 `Nothing changes from what the room has now` |
| `server/tasks.js` | `onChange(fn)` | `fn(roomId)` after a change only: a no-op `ensureDay` (nothing made, missed or cancelled) must **not** call it, or every page GET would publish and re-fetch for ever |
| `server/tasks.js` | rows | the harvest rules' `unit` is `'kg'`; `roomView().upkeep[i].dueDate`; done/skip/cancel return the closed row; `addLongterm` returns the row, `addFromSuggestion` the rows in the order of `machines` |
| `server/tasks.js` | `logCsv(roomId)` | `\n` line ends, header first, then one line per closed row; fields quoted only when they hold `,` `"` or a line break (RFC 4180); time `HH:MM` KL; amount as JS prints it (`3.5`), empty when null |
| validation | — | part B checks the bodies before calling `tasks` (worker, reason, amount, text, note, suggestion shape), so part A may but need not repeat those checks |

Also owned here and touching other parts: `server/main.js` creates the one `tasks` and passes it to `createApp` **and** to
`createController({ …, tasks })` (B7). The controller's use of it (the daily memo) is the controller part's; until that
lands the extra option is ignored by `createController`'s destructuring.

## Decisions made here (flag to the lead)

| Point | Choice |
|---|---|
| Body of `PUT /api/workers` | `{ workers: [...] }` (the reply shape) or a bare array, both accepted |
| `PUT /api/task-rules` | `builtin` and `custom` each optional; a given `custom` replaces the whole list; a given `builtin` entry changes only the keys it names (others keep their stored values); `everyDays` on a daily built-in is ignored; `null` on an upkeep built-in = back to the default |
| Field names of list errors | dotted like `days.${stage}` in `program.js`: `workers.1.name`, `builtin.acFilter.everyDays`, `custom.0.rooms`, `suggestion.machines` |
| Making rows on change | `GET` room tasks → `tasks.ensureDay(id)`; `GET` Today → `tasks.ensureAll(today)`; `PUT /api/task-rules` → `ensureAll(today)` so a rule turned on or added shows at once. No `ensureDay` on the crop or room PUT (spec section 4 "When"; and the existing `live-sse` tests pin those routes' events exactly) — the Tasks card should re-fetch on `room` parts `crop` and `room` too |
| Today's top-bar total | not added to the reply; the web sums `rooms[].counts.due` (and then follows the `tasks {due}` event) |
| Limits | worker name 40, reason 200, own task text 1–120 (as custom rules), note 500, amount 0–10,000, a suggestion's from/to 0–1,000,000 |
| Status of `POST /api/rooms/:id/tasks` | 201 (as `POST /api/rooms`) |
| Download name | `attachment; filename="<room name> tasks.csv"` for a plain-ASCII name; otherwise an ASCII stand-in (`_` for others and for `"` `\`) plus `filename*=UTF-8''…` (Node refuses non-Latin-1 header text) |

```js
// The tasks over HTTP (spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md section 6): a room's tasks, Today, adding long-term tasks (own and
// Plan this), Done, Skip and Cancel with their errors.

import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { openDb } from '../server/db.js';
import { createApp } from '../server/app.js';
import { localDate, addDays } from '../server/task-rules.js';

const ROOM = { name: 'Room 1', length: 6, width: 4, height: 2.5 };

async function app(t, { controller = null } = {}) {
  const db = openDb(':memory:');
  const server = createApp(db, { controller });
  await new Promise((r) => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', r));
  t.after(async () => { await new Promise((r) => server.close(r)); db.close(); });
  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
  const call = async (method, path, body) => {
    const res = await fetch(base + path, { method, headers: body ? { 'content-type': 'application/json' } : {}, body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined });
    const text = await res.text();
    return { status: res.status, body: text ? JSON.parse(text) : null };
  };
  return { db, call };
}

// A room with grey oyster blocks, fruiting from now (the crop PUT starts the stage). → its id
async function fruitingRoom(call, name = 'Room 1') {
  const made = await call('POST', '/api/rooms', { ...ROOM, name });
  assert.equal((await call('PUT', `/api/rooms/${made.body.id}/crop`, { crops: [{ crop: 'oyster', blocks: 100 }] })).status, 200);
  return made.body.id;
}

const byRule = (rows, key) => rows.find((r) => r.ruleKey === key);
const hhmm = (ms) => new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', { timeZone: 'Asia/Kuala_Lumpur', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hourCycle: 'h23' }).format(ms);

test("GET a room's tasks makes today's rows: the fruiting oyster day, the upkeep due today, no long-term yet; twice makes no more", async (t) => {
  const { call } = await app(t);
  const id = await fruitingRoom(call);
  const r = await call('GET', `/api/rooms/${id}/tasks`);

  assert.deepEqual(r.body.today.map((x) => x.ruleKey).sort(), ['contamWalk', 'floorClean', 'footbath', 'harvestAm', 'harvestPm', 'humidifierCheck']);
  assert.ok(r.body.today.every((x) => x.kind === 'daily' && x.status === 'open' && x.roomId === id && x.date === r.body.date));
  assert.deepEqual(r.body.upkeep.map((x) => x.ruleKey).sort(), ['acChemWash', 'acFilter', 'acService', 'flyTraps', 'humidifierDescale']);
  assert.deepEqual(r.body.longterm, []);
  assert.deepEqual(r.body.counts, { due: 11, overdue: 0, longterm: 0 });
  const again = await call('GET', `/api/rooms/${id}/tasks`);
  assert.deepEqual(again.body.today.map((x) => x.id), r.body.today.map((x) => x.id));
  assert.deepEqual(again.body.upkeep.map((x) => x.id), r.body.upkeep.map((x) => x.id));
});
```
