- [ ] **Step 3: Write the minimal implementation**

Create `server/tasks-api.js`:

```js
// The task routes (spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md section 6), called from api.js: GET and POST /api/rooms/:id/tasks,
// GET /api/rooms/:id/tasks/log and /log.csv, GET /api/tasks/today, POST /api/tasks/:id/done|skip|cancel, GET and PUT
// /api/workers and /api/task-rules. server/tasks.js makes and closes the rows; this checks what the pages send.

import { localDate } from './task-rules.js';

const ROOM_TASKS = /^\/api\/rooms\/([1-9][0-9]{0,14})\/tasks$/;

// db: the farm database; tasks: from createTasks (server/tasks.js). now is injectable.
// → async (req, res, path, { send, readJson, HttpError, url }) that answers true when the path is one of its routes.
export function createTaskRoutes({ db, tasks, now = Date.now }) {
  async function route(req, res, path, { send, readJson, HttpError, url }) {
    const notFound = () => new HttpError(404, 'Not found');
    const roomOf = (text) => {
      const room = db.getRoom(Number(text));
      if (!room) throw new HttpError(404, 'Room not found');
      return room;
    };

    if (path === '/api/tasks/today') {
      if (req.method !== 'GET') throw notFound();
      tasks.ensureAll(localDate(now()));
      send(res, 200, tasks.todayView());
      return true;
    }
    const roomTasks = ROOM_TASKS.exec(path);
    if (roomTasks) {
      if (req.method !== 'GET') throw notFound();
      const room = roomOf(roomTasks[1]);
      tasks.ensureDay(room.id);
      send(res, 200, tasks.roomView(room.id));
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  // A 4xx thrown by server/tasks.js or by a body check here (status, message, maybe field) becomes the API's HttpError.
  return async function taskRoutes(req, res, path, helpers) {
    try {
      return await route(req, res, path, helpers);
    } catch (err) {
      const { HttpError } = helpers;
      if (!(err instanceof HttpError) && Number.isInteger(err?.status) && err.status >= 400 && err.status < 500) {
        throw new HttpError(err.status, err.message, err.field);
      }
      throw err;
    }
  };
}
```

```js
  assert.equal((await call('POST', `/api/rooms/${id}/tasks`, { text: 'Paint the walls', note: '' })).body.rows[0].note, null);

  const noMoney = await post({ machines: good.machines }); // rmMonth and suggestedAt left out: none, and now

  assert.equal(noMoney.body.rows[0].source.rmMonth, null);
```

In `server/tasks-api.js`, after `const ROOM_TASKS = …;` add:

```js
export const TEXT_MAX = 120; // an own long-term task and an own rule
export const NOTE_MAX = 500;
export const MACHINE_KEYS = ['fanFlow', 'acBTU', 'humidifierLH', 'dehumidifierLday']; // the sizing keys
const MACHINE_MAX = 1_000_000;
const MACHINES_ERROR = 'Machines must be a list of fan, aircon, humidifier or dehumidifier changes, each from and to a number from 0 to 1,000,000';

// A wrong value in a body: a 400 with the field (taskRoutes turns it into the API's HttpError).
class TaskInputError extends Error {
  constructor(message, field) {
    super(message);
    this.status = 400;
    this.field = field;
  }
}

const isObject = (v) => v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v);
const trimmed = (v) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v.trim() : '');
const isNumberIn = (v, min, max) => typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) && v >= min && v <= max;

// An own long-term task: { text, note? } → { text, note|null }.
function readOwn(body) {
  const text = trimmed(body.text);
  if (text === '' || text.length > TEXT_MAX) throw new TaskInputError(`Task text must be 1 to ${TEXT_MAX} characters`, 'text');
  const given = body.note ?? null;
  if (given !== null && (typeof given !== 'string' || given.trim().length > NOTE_MAX)) {
    throw new TaskInputError(`Note must be ${NOTE_MAX} characters or fewer`, 'note');
  }
  const note = trimmed(given);
  return { text, note: note === '' ? null : note };
}

// Plan this: { machines: [{ key, from, to }], rmMonth?, suggestedAt? } → the same, rmMonth null and suggestedAt `at` when
// left out. Whether anything changes is server/tasks.js's to say.
function readSuggestion(s, at) {
  if (!isObject(s)) throw new TaskInputError('Suggestion must be an object', 'suggestion');
  if (!Array.isArray(s.machines) || s.machines.length === 0 || s.machines.length > MACHINE_KEYS.length) {
    throw new TaskInputError(MACHINES_ERROR, 'suggestion.machines');
  }
  const seen = new Set();
  const machines = s.machines.map((m) => {
    const ok = isObject(m) && MACHINE_KEYS.includes(m.key) && !seen.has(m.key) && isNumberIn(m.from, 0, MACHINE_MAX) && isNumberIn(m.to, 0, MACHINE_MAX);
    if (!ok) throw new TaskInputError(MACHINES_ERROR, 'suggestion.machines');
    seen.add(m.key);
    return { key: m.key, from: m.from, to: m.to };
  });
  const rmMonth = s.rmMonth ?? null;
  if (rmMonth !== null && !isNumberIn(rmMonth, -Number.MAX_VALUE, Number.MAX_VALUE)) {
    throw new TaskInputError('RM a month must be a number or empty', 'suggestion.rmMonth');
  }
  const suggestedAt = s.suggestedAt ?? at;
  if (!Number.isInteger(suggestedAt) || suggestedAt < 0) {
    throw new TaskInputError('Suggested at must be a time in milliseconds', 'suggestion.suggestedAt');
  }
  return { machines, rmMonth, suggestedAt };
}
```

```js
        : [tasks.addLongterm(room.id, readOwn(body))];
      send(res, 201, { rows });
      return true;
    }
```

```js
// Who did it (Done, Skip): a name of 1 to WORKER_MAX characters.
function readWorker(body) {
  const worker = trimmed(body.worker);
  if (worker === '') throw new TaskInputError('Pick who did it', 'worker');
  if (worker.length > WORKER_MAX) throw new TaskInputError(`Name must be ${WORKER_MAX} characters or fewer`, 'worker');
  return worker;
}

// Why (Skip, Cancel): 1 to REASON_MAX characters.
function readReason(body) {
  const reason = trimmed(body.reason);
  if (reason === '') throw new TaskInputError('Say why', 'reason');
  if (reason.length > REASON_MAX) throw new TaskInputError(`Reason must be ${REASON_MAX} characters or fewer`, 'reason');
  return reason;
}

// The amount on Done: a number from 0 to AMOUNT_MAX, or null when left out or empty.
function readAmount(body) {
  const amount = body.amount;
  if (amount === undefined || amount === null || amount === '') return null;
  if (!isNumberIn(amount, 0, AMOUNT_MAX)) throw new TaskInputError('Amount must be a number from 0 to 10,000', 'amount');
  return amount;
}
```

after `readAmount` add:

```js
// The log's ?before=<id>: null when left out.
function readBefore(url) {
  const text = url.searchParams.get('before');
  if (text === null || text === '') return null;
  if (!/^[1-9][0-9]{0,14}$/.test(text)) throw new TaskInputError('before must be a task id', 'before');
  return Number(text);
}

// The Content-Disposition of a download. A header holds Latin-1 only: a name with anything outside printable ASCII (or a
// quote or backslash) gets an ASCII stand-in plus the exact name as UTF-8 (RFC 6266 / 5987).
export function attachment(filename) {
  const ascii = filename.replace(/[^\x20-\x7e]|["\\]/g, '_');
  if (ascii === filename) return `attachment; filename="${filename}"`;
  const utf8 = encodeURIComponent(filename).replace(/['()*]/g, (c) => `%${c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).toUpperCase()}`);
  return `attachment; filename="${ascii}"; filename*=UTF-8''${utf8}`;
}
```

Inside `route`, just before the final `return false;` add:

```js

      }
      send(res, 200, { rows: tasks.log(room.id, { before: readBefore(url) }) });
```

```js
  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
  const call = async (method, path, body) => {
    const res = await fetch(base + path, { method, headers: body ? { 'content-type': 'application/json' } : {}, body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined });
    const text = await res.text();
    return { status: res.status, body: text ? JSON.parse(text) : null };
  };
  return { db, call };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the minimal implementation**

In `server/tasks-api.js`, after `readBefore` add:

```js
// PUT /api/workers: { workers: [{ id?, name, active? }] } or the bare list → [{ id|null, name, active }]. existing: the stored
// workers (an id must be one of theirs, once). Names are kept trimmed and must differ, case aside.
function readWorkers(body, existing) {
  const list = Array.isArray(body) ? body : isObject(body) ? body.workers : undefined;
  if (!Array.isArray(list)) throw new TaskInputError('Workers must be a list', 'workers');
  const ids = new Set(existing.map((w) => w.id));
  const usedIds = new Set();
  const names = new Set();
  return list.map((w, i) => {
    if (!isObject(w)) throw new TaskInputError('Each worker must be an object with a name', `workers.${i}`);
    let id = null;
    if (w.id !== undefined && w.id !== null) {
      if (!ids.has(w.id) || usedIds.has(w.id)) throw new TaskInputError('Unknown worker', `workers.${i}.id`);
      usedIds.add(w.id);
      id = w.id;
    }
    const name = trimmed(w.name);
    if (name === '') throw new TaskInputError('Name is required', `workers.${i}.name`);
    if (name.length > WORKER_MAX) throw new TaskInputError(`Name must be ${WORKER_MAX} characters or fewer`, `workers.${i}.name`);
    if (names.has(name.toLowerCase())) throw new TaskInputError(`A worker called "${name}" already exists`, `workers.${i}.name`);
    names.add(name.toLowerCase());
    const active = w.active ?? true;
    if (typeof active !== 'boolean') throw new TaskInputError('Active must be true or false', `workers.${i}.active`);
    return { id, name, active };
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the minimal implementation**

In `server/tasks-api.js` replace line 5 (`import { localDate } from './task-rules.js';`) with:

```js
import { DAILY_RULES, CLEAR_OUT, UPKEEP_RULES, SLOTS, localDate } from './task-rules.js';
```

After `readWorkers` add:

```js
// The built-in rules in the Settings card's order (daily, the done program's, upkeep), each once.
const BUILTIN = [...new Map([...DAILY_RULES, CLEAR_OUT, ...UPKEEP_RULES].map((r) => [r.key, r])).values()];
const BUILTIN_BY_KEY = new Map(BUILTIN.map((r) => [r.key, r]));
const INTERVAL_MAX = 365;
const INTERVAL_ERROR = `Interval must be a whole number from 1 to ${INTERVAL_MAX} days`;
const CUSTOM_INTERVAL_ERROR = `Interval must be daily or a whole number from 1 to ${INTERVAL_MAX} days`;
const ROOMS_ERROR = "Rooms must be every room, or one or more of this farm's rooms";
const SLOT_ERROR = `Time of day must be ${SLOTS.slice(0, -1).join(', ')} or ${SLOTS.at(-1)}`;
const isInterval = (v) => Number.isInteger(v) && v >= 1 && v <= INTERVAL_MAX;

// GET /api/task-rules: every built-in rule with the owner's on/off and interval, and the owner's own rules.
// stored: db.taskRuleSettings(); custom: db.customTaskRules().
function rulesView(stored, custom) {
  const byKey = new Map(stored.map((s) => [s.key, s]));
  const builtin = BUILTIN.map((rule) => {
    const s = byKey.get(rule.key);
    const upkeep = rule.kind === 'upkeep';
    return {
      key: rule.key, kind: rule.kind, text: rule.text,
      families: rule.families ?? null, stages: rule.stages ?? null, when: rule.when ?? null, slot: rule.slot ?? 'any',
      everyDays: upkeep ? s?.everyDays ?? rule.everyDays : null,
      enabled: s?.enabled ?? true,
    };
  });
  return { builtin, custom };
}

// PUT /api/task-rules: { builtin?: [{ key, enabled?, everyDays? }], custom?: [{ id?, text, rooms, everyDays, slot, enabled }] }
// → { settings: one entry per built-in key (the stored ones kept where the body says nothing) | null, custom: the new list | null }.
// roomIds: the farm's room ids; customIds: the stored own rules' ids.
function readRules(body, { stored, customIds, roomIds }) {
  if (!isObject(body)) throw new TaskInputError('Body must be a JSON object');
  let settings = null;
  if (body.builtin !== undefined) {
    if (!Array.isArray(body.builtin)) throw new TaskInputError('Built-in rules must be a list', 'builtin');
    const byKey = new Map(stored.map((s) => [s.key, s]));
    const next = new Map(BUILTIN.map((r) => [r.key, { key: r.key, enabled: byKey.get(r.key)?.enabled ?? true, everyDays: byKey.get(r.key)?.everyDays ?? null }]));
    for (const entry of body.builtin) {
      const key = isObject(entry) ? entry.key : entry;
      const rule = isObject(entry) && typeof key === 'string' ? BUILTIN_BY_KEY.get(key) : undefined;
      if (!rule) throw new TaskInputError(`Unknown rule ${JSON.stringify(key ?? null)}`, 'builtin');
      const s = next.get(rule.key);
      if (entry.enabled !== undefined) {
        if (typeof entry.enabled !== 'boolean') throw new TaskInputError('On must be true or false', `builtin.${rule.key}.enabled`);
        s.enabled = entry.enabled;
      }
      if (rule.kind === 'upkeep' && entry.everyDays !== undefined) { // a daily rule's interval is ignored
        if (entry.everyDays !== null && !isInterval(entry.everyDays)) throw new TaskInputError(INTERVAL_ERROR, `builtin.${rule.key}.everyDays`);
        s.everyDays = entry.everyDays;
      }
    }
    settings = [...next.values()];
  }
  let custom = null;
  if (body.custom !== undefined) {
    if (!Array.isArray(body.custom)) throw new TaskInputError('Your own rules must be a list', 'custom');
    const usedIds = new Set();
    custom = body.custom.map((c, i) => {
      const at = (field) => `custom.${i}.${field}`;
      if (!isObject(c)) throw new TaskInputError('Each rule must be an object', `custom.${i}`);
      let id = null;
      if (c.id !== undefined && c.id !== null) {
        if (!customIds.has(c.id) || usedIds.has(c.id)) throw new TaskInputError('Unknown rule', at('id'));
        usedIds.add(c.id);
        id = c.id;
      }
      const text = trimmed(c.text);
      if (text === '' || text.length > TEXT_MAX) throw new TaskInputError(`Task text must be 1 to ${TEXT_MAX} characters`, at('text'));
      const rooms = c.rooms ?? null;
      if (rooms !== null && !(Array.isArray(rooms) && rooms.length > 0 && rooms.every((r) => roomIds.has(r)) && new Set(rooms).size === rooms.length)) {
        throw new TaskInputError(ROOMS_ERROR, at('rooms'));
      }
      const everyDays = c.everyDays ?? null;
      if (everyDays !== null && !isInterval(everyDays)) throw new TaskInputError(CUSTOM_INTERVAL_ERROR, at('everyDays'));
      const slot = c.slot ?? 'any';
      if (!SLOTS.includes(slot)) throw new TaskInputError(SLOT_ERROR, at('slot'));
      const enabled = c.enabled ?? true;
      if (typeof enabled !== 'boolean') throw new TaskInputError('On must be true or false', at('enabled'));
      return { id, text, rooms, everyDays, slot, enabled };
    });
  }
  return { settings, custom };
}
```

```js
        return true;
      }
      if (req.method !== 'PUT') throw notFound();
```

```js
  assert.deepEqual(got, []);
  await call('POST', `/api/tasks/${byRule(view.today, 'harvestAm').id}/done`, { worker: 'Ali', amount: 1 });
  assert.deepEqual(got.slice(-2), [['room', { id, part: 'tasks' }], ['tasks', { due: view.counts.due - 1 }]]);
  got.length = 0;
  await call('POST', `/api/tasks/${byRule(view.upkeep, 'flyTraps').id}/skip`, { worker: 'Ali', reason: 'None in stock' });
  assert.deepEqual(got.slice(-2), [['room', { id, part: 'tasks' }], ['tasks', { due: view.counts.due - 2 }]]);
  got.length = 0;
  await call('POST', `/api/rooms/${id}/tasks`, { text: 'Fix the door seal' });
  assert.ok(got.length >= 2 && onlyTasks(got, id));
  assert.deepEqual(got.at(-1), ['tasks', { due: view.counts.due - 2 }]);
  got.length = 0;
  assert.equal((await call('POST', `/api/tasks/${byRule(view.today, 'harvestAm').id}/done`, { worker: 'Ali' })).status, 409);
  assert.equal((await call('POST', `/api/tasks/${byRule(view.today, 'harvestPm').id}/done`, { worker: '' })).status, 400);
  assert.deepEqual(got, []); // a refused change publishes nothing
});

test("the controller's daily making (ensureDay outside a route) publishes too", async (t) => {
  const { db, tasks, got } = await app(t);
  const id = db.createRoom(ROOM).id; // no crops: footbath, floor and the five upkeep rows
  tasks.ensureDay(id);
  assert.ok(onlyTasks(got, id));
  assert.deepEqual(got.slice(-2), [['room', { id, part: 'tasks' }], ['tasks', { due: 7 }]]);
  got.length = 0;
  tasks.ensureDay(id);
  assert.deepEqual(got, []);
});

test('an own rule added in Settings makes its row in every room at once and publishes', async (t) => {
  const { db, tasks, call, got } = await app(t);
  const a = db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, name: 'Room A' }).id;
  const b = db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, name: 'Room B' }).id;
  tasks.ensureAll((await call('GET', '/api/tasks/today')).body.date);
  got.length = 0;
  await call('PUT', '/api/task-rules', { custom: [{ text: 'Check the water tank', rooms: null, everyDays: null, slot: 'morning', enabled: true }] });
  for (const id of [a, b]) assert.ok(got.some(([type, data]) => type === 'room' && data.id === id && data.part === 'tasks'));
  assert.deepEqual(got.at(-1), ['tasks', { due: 16 }]);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the module**

Create `web/tasks-math.js` (the imports are used by C2 and C3; they load nothing with side effects):

```js
// Pure helpers for the Tasks tab, the Today page, Plan this on Suggest machines and the Settings task rules (spec
// docs/specs/2026-10-30-tasks-design.md section 7; no DOM, so they can be tested in Node). Days are the farm's
// local days ('YYYY-MM-DD', Asia/Kuala_Lumpur) as the server sends them; times show on the farm's clock whatever the
// browser's time zone.

import { STAGES } from './crops.js';
import { monthly } from './suggest-math.js';
import { fieldTexts, parseRoomFields, roomBody } from './room-fields.js';

export const FARM_TZ = 'Asia/Kuala_Lumpur';
export const SLOTS = ['morning', 'afternoon', 'any'];
export const SLOT_LABELS = { morning: 'Morning', afternoon: 'Afternoon', any: 'Any time' };
export const LOG_DAYS = 14; // the Log shows the closed tasks of the last 14 days, then Older…
export const LOG_PAGE = 50; // GET /api/rooms/:id/tasks/log answers 50 a page
const MONTHS = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
const WEEKDAYS = ['Sunday', 'Monday', 'Tuesday', 'Wednesday', 'Thursday', 'Friday', 'Saturday'];
const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
const finite = (v) => typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v);
const grouped = (v) => v.toLocaleString('en-US', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
const plural = (n, one, many = `${one}s`) => `${n} ${n === 1 ? one : many}`;

// ---- days and times ----

const PARTS = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
  timeZone: FARM_TZ, year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hourCycle: 'h23',
});
function farmParts(ms) {
  const p = Object.fromEntries(PARTS.formatToParts(ms).map((x) => [x.type, x.value]));
  return { date: `${p.year}-${p.month}-${p.day}`, time: `${p.hour}:${p.minute}` };
}
export const farmDate = (ms) => farmParts(ms).date;
export const farmTime = (ms) => farmParts(ms).time;
const utcOf = (date) => { const [y, m, d] = date.split('-').map(Number); return Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d); };
export const dayMonth = (date) => { const [, m, d] = date.split('-').map(Number); return `${d} ${MONTHS[m - 1]}`; };
export const dayTitle = (date) => `${WEEKDAYS[new Date(utcOf(date)).getUTCDay()]} ${dayMonth(date)}`;
export const daysFrom = (a, b) => Math.round((utcOf(b) - utcOf(a)) / DAY_MS);

// ---- counts ----

export const tasksDueWords = (n) => (n > 0 ? `${plural(n, 'task')} due` : 'Nothing due');
export const longtermWords = (n) => plural(n, 'long-term task');
export const todayLinkLabel = (n) => (n > 0 ? `Today: ${tasksDueWords(n)}` : 'Today');

// The Tasks tab's dot (spec 7.1): counts.due (the stage tasks open today and the upkeep due or overdue), amber with an
// overdue one; null: no dot. Long-term tasks never make a dot.
export function tabDot(counts) {
  const due = finite(counts?.due) ? counts.due : 0;
  if (due <= 0) return null;
  const overdue = finite(counts.overdue) ? counts.overdue : 0;
  return { text: due > 99 ? '99+' : String(due), level: overdue > 0 ? 'warning' : 'due', label: `${tasksDueWords(due)}${overdue > 0 ? `, ${overdue} overdue` : ''}` };
}

// The Today link's number: every room's counts.due (the open rows when a room has no counts). null: not a Today reply.
export function todayCount(view) {
  if (!Array.isArray(view?.rooms)) return null;
  const open = (rows) => (rows ?? []).filter((r) => r.status === 'open').length;
  return view.rooms.reduce((n, r) => n + (finite(r.counts?.due) ? r.counts.due : open(r.today) + open(r.upkeepDue)), 0);
}

// ---- rows ----

const STATUS_WORDS = { open: 'Open', done: 'Done', skipped: 'Skipped', missed: 'Missed', cancelled: 'Cancelled' };
const CLOSED = ['done', 'skipped', 'missed', 'cancelled'];

// The closed rows of a slot in words: "6 done", "2 done · 1 skipped".
export function foldLine(rows) {
  return CLOSED.map((s) => [s, rows.filter((r) => r.status === s).length]).filter(([, n]) => n > 0).map(([s, n]) => `${n} ${s}`).join(' · ');
}

// Today's daily rows by Morning, Afternoon and Any time (a slot with no rows is left out): [{ slot, label, open, closed, fold }].
export function slotGroups(rows) {
  return SLOTS.map((slot) => {
    const mine = (rows ?? []).filter((r) => (SLOTS.includes(r.slot) ? r.slot : 'any') === slot);
    const closed = mine.filter((r) => r.status !== 'open');
    return { slot, label: SLOT_LABELS[slot], open: mine.filter((r) => r.status === 'open'), closed, fold: foldLine(closed) };
  }).filter((g) => g.open.length + g.closed.length > 0);
}

export const amountText = (row) => (finite(row?.amount) ? `${grouped(row.amount)}${row.unit ? ` ${row.unit}` : ''}` : '');

// A closed row in one line: "Done by Ali at 09:12 · 2.4 kg", "Skipped by Siti at 10:05 · No bags opened yet", "Missed".
export function closedLine(row) {
  if (row.status === 'missed') return 'Missed';
  const head = `${STATUS_WORDS[row.status] ?? row.status}${row.worker ? ` by ${row.worker}` : ''}${finite(row.closedAt) ? ` at ${farmTime(row.closedAt)}` : ''}`;
  return [head, amountText(row), row.reason ?? ''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
}

// A rule that records an amount (the harvest's kg, the bags taken out) gives its open rows a unit or a label.
export const recordsAmount = (row) => Boolean(row?.unit || row?.amountLabel);
export function amountField(row) {
  const label = row.amountLabel || 'Amount';
  const unit = row.unit || null;
  return { label: unit && !label.toLowerCase().includes(unit.toLowerCase()) ? `${label} (${unit})` : label, unit };
}

// An upkeep row's due words (spec 7.1): "overdue 3 days" (amber), "due today", "due 2 Nov"; group: overdue, soon (within
// 7 days) or later; late: days past the due date (negative before it).
const dueDateOf = (row) => row.dueDate ?? (finite(row.dueAt) ? farmDate(row.dueAt) : null);
export function dueWords(row, today) {
  const due = dueDateOf(row);
  if (!due) return { text: '', tone: null, group: 'later', late: null };
  const late = daysFrom(due, today);
  if (late > 0) return { text: `overdue ${plural(late, 'day')}`, tone: 'amber', group: 'overdue', late };
  if (late === 0) return { text: 'due today', tone: null, group: 'soon', late };
  return { text: `due ${dayMonth(due)}`, tone: null, group: late >= -7 ? 'soon' : 'later', late };
}

// The Upkeep part's open rows: overdue first, then due within 7 days, then the rest, each by date. → [{ row, due }]
export function upkeepRows(rows, today) {
  const order = { overdue: 0, soon: 1, later: 2 };
  return (rows ?? []).filter((r) => r.status === 'open').map((row) => ({ row, due: dueWords(row, today) }))
    .sort((a, b) => order[a.due.group] - order[b.due.group] || (b.due.late ?? -1e9) - (a.due.late ?? -1e9) || a.row.id - b.row.id);
}
```

```js
  parseAmount, checkReason, checkLongterm, logRows, logLine,
} from '../web/tasks-math.js';

test('a long-term row from Suggest machines in one line', () => {
  const row = { text: 'Aircon 9,000 → 12,000 BTU/h', source: { machine: 'acBTU', from: 9000, to: 12000, rmMonth: -42.4, suggestedAt: at(14, 0, 29) } };
  assert.equal(longtermLine(row), 'Aircon 9,000 → 12,000 BTU/h · RM 42 a month less · from Suggest machines, 29 Oct');
  assert.equal(longtermLine({ ...row, source: { ...row.source, rmMonth: 15.6 } }), 'Aircon 9,000 → 12,000 BTU/h · RM 16 a month more · from Suggest machines, 29 Oct');
  assert.equal(longtermLine({ ...row, source: { ...row.source, rmMonth: null } }), 'Aircon 9,000 → 12,000 BTU/h · from Suggest machines, 29 Oct', 'no electricity price: no money');
  assert.equal(longtermLine({ text: 'Fix the door seal', source: null }), 'Fix the door seal');
  assert.equal(rmWords(0.3), 'about the same money a month');
  assert.equal(rmWords(null), '');
});

test('a machine change in words, as the server names the task', () => {
  assert.equal(changeText({ key: 'fanFlow', from: 150, to: 200 }), 'Fan 150 → 200 m³/h');
  assert.equal(changeText({ key: 'acBTU', from: 9000, to: 12000 }), 'Aircon 9,000 → 12,000 BTU/h');
  assert.equal(changeText({ key: 'humidifierLH', from: 0.5, to: 1 }), 'Humidifier 0.5 → 1 L/h');
  assert.equal(changeText({ key: 'dehumidifierLday', from: 0, to: 10 }), 'Dehumidifier none → 10 L/day');
  assert.equal(changeText({ key: 'dehumidifierLday', from: 10, to: 0 }), 'Dehumidifier 10 L/day → none');
  const now = { fanFlow: 150, acBTU: 9000, humidifierLH: 0.5, dehumidifierLday: 0 };
  assert.deepEqual(machineChanges(now, { ...now, acBTU: 12000, dehumidifierLday: 10 }).map((c) => [c.key, c.from, c.to]), [['acBTU', 9000, 12000], ['dehumidifierLday', 0, 10]]);
  assert.deepEqual(machineChanges(now, { ...now }), []);
});

test('Plan this: the changes against the baseline and the RM a month against it', () => {
  const result = {
    days: 2,
    baseline: { machines: { fanFlow: 150, acBTU: 9000, humidifierLH: 0.5, dehumidifierLday: 0 }, kWh: 20, litres: 0 },
    best: [{ machines: { fanFlow: 150, acBTU: 12000, humidifierLH: 1, dehumidifierLday: 0 }, kWh: 15, litres: 0 }],
  };
  const plan = planOf(result, 0, { electricity: 0.55, water: null }, 123);
  assert.deepEqual(plan.changes.map((c) => c.text), ['Aircon 9,000 → 12,000 BTU/h', 'Humidifier 0.5 → 1 L/h']);
  assert.equal(plan.rmMonth, -41.25, 'RM 123.75 a month against RM 165');
  assert.equal(plan.rmText, 'RM 41 a month less');
  assert.deepEqual(plan.body, { suggestion: { machines: [{ key: 'acBTU', from: 9000, to: 12000 }, { key: 'humidifierLH', from: 0.5, to: 1 }], rmMonth: -41.25, suggestedAt: 123 } });
  assert.equal(planOf(result, 0, { electricity: null, water: null }, 123).rmMonth, null, 'no price: no money');
  assert.equal(planOf({ ...result, baseline: undefined }, 0, { electricity: 0.55 }, 123), null, 'no baseline: the "from" is unknown');
});

const ROOM = {
  name: 'Room 2', length: 3, width: 2, height: 2.5, trhAddress: 1, co2Address: 2, meterAddress: null, dpAddress: null, relayAddress: 3,
  acAddress: null, dehumidifier: false, lights: false, intakeW: 60, exhaustW: 60, circulationW: 60, humidifierW: 80, humidifierLH: 2, acInputW: 800,
  dehumidifierW: 380, dehumidifierLday: 20, lightsW: 60, fanMaxPa: 200, filterCleanPa: 40, filterFinalPa: 80, filterChangeDays: 30, gateway: null,
};

test('Done on a long-term machine task offers these Setup values (spec 7.5)', () => {
  assert.equal(acInputOf(12000), 1070, '12,000 ÷ 11.25 = 1,066.7 W, to 10 W');
  assert.equal(acInputOf(9000), 800);
  assert.equal(acInputOf(18000), 1600);
  assert.deepEqual(setupUpdate({ machine: 'acBTU', from: 9000, to: 12000 }, ROOM), [
    { name: 'acInputW', label: 'Aircon rated input', unit: 'W', kind: 'number', before: 800, after: 1070 },
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(setupUpdate({ machine: 'humidifierLH', from: 2, to: 3 }, ROOM), [
    { name: 'humidifierLH', label: 'Humidifier output', unit: 'L/h', kind: 'number', before: 2, after: 3 },
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(setupUpdate({ machine: 'dehumidifierLday', from: 0, to: 25 }, ROOM), [
    { name: 'dehumidifier', label: 'Dehumidifier fitted', unit: null, kind: 'check', before: false, after: true },
    { name: 'dehumidifierLday', label: 'Dehumidifier capacity', unit: 'L/day', kind: 'number', before: null, after: 25 },
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(setupUpdate({ machine: 'dehumidifierLday', from: 20, to: 0 }, { ...ROOM, dehumidifier: true }), [
    { name: 'dehumidifier', label: 'Dehumidifier fitted', unit: null, kind: 'check', before: true, after: false },
  ]);
  assert.equal(setupUpdate({ machine: 'fanFlow', from: 150, to: 200 }, ROOM), null, 'Setup keeps no fan flow');
  assert.equal(setupUpdate(null, ROOM), null);
  assert.equal(beforeText({ kind: 'number', unit: 'W', before: 1050 }), '1,050 W');
  assert.equal(beforeText({ kind: 'number', unit: 'L/day', before: null }), 'not set');
  assert.equal(beforeText({ kind: 'check', before: false }), 'not fitted');
});

test("Save and mark done: the room's PUT body with the edited values, checked as Setup checks them", () => {
  const { body } = setupBody(ROOM, { acInputW: '1070' });
  assert.equal(body.acInputW, 1070);
  assert.equal(body.name, 'Room 2');
  assert.equal(body.humidifierLH, 2, 'the rest as the room has it');
  const fitted = setupBody(ROOM, { dehumidifier: true, dehumidifierLday: '25' }).body;
  assert.equal(fitted.dehumidifier, true);
  assert.equal(fitted.dehumidifierLday, 25);
  assert.deepEqual(setupBody(ROOM, { acInputW: 'abc' }), { errors: { acInputW: 'Aircon rated input must be a number from 0 to 20000 W' } });
});

test('the boxes of the dialogs: an amount, a reason, a long-term task', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(parseAmount('', 'kg'), { value: null });
  assert.deepEqual(parseAmount(' 2.45 ', 'kg'), { value: 2.45 });
  assert.deepEqual(parseAmount('10000', 'kg'), { value: 10000 });
  for (const t of ['10000.5', '-1', 'abc', '1e3', '2.456']) {
    assert.deepEqual(parseAmount(t, 'kg'), { error: 'Amount must be a number from 0 to 10,000 kg, or empty' }, t);
  }
  assert.deepEqual(checkReason('  '), { error: 'Say why' });
  assert.deepEqual(checkReason(' Bags not ready '), { value: 'Bags not ready' });
  assert.deepEqual(checkReason('x'.repeat(201)), { error: 'At most 200 characters' });
  assert.deepEqual(checkLongterm({ text: ' ', note: '' }), { errors: { text: 'Say what the task is' } });
  assert.deepEqual(checkLongterm({ text: 'x'.repeat(121), note: '' }), { errors: { text: 'At most 120 characters' } });
  assert.deepEqual(checkLongterm({ text: ' Seal the door ', note: ' ' }), { values: { text: 'Seal the door', note: null } });
  assert.deepEqual(checkLongterm({ text: 'Seal the door', note: 'x'.repeat(501) }), { errors: { note: 'At most 500 characters' } });
});

test('the Log: the last 14 days, each line with its time, status, who, amount and why', () => {
```

```js
// ---- long-term tasks and Plan this (spec 7.1 and 7.4) ----

export const MACHINE_ORDER = ['fanFlow', 'acBTU', 'humidifierLH', 'dehumidifierLday'];
const MACHINE_WORDS = { fanFlow: ['Fan', 'm³/h'], acBTU: ['Aircon', 'BTU/h'], humidifierLH: ['Humidifier', 'L/h'], dehumidifierLday: ['Dehumidifier', 'L/day'] };

// A suggestion's RM a month against the room's now, in words: "RM 42 a month less", "RM 16 a month more"; '' when unknown.
export function rmWords(rm) {
  if (!finite(rm)) return '';
  const whole = Math.round(Math.abs(rm));
  return whole === 0 ? 'about the same money a month' : `RM ${grouped(whole)} a month ${rm < 0 ? 'less' : 'more'}`;
}

// A long-term row in one line: "Aircon 9,000 → 12,000 BTU/h · RM 42 a month less · from Suggest machines, 29 Oct".
export function longtermLine(row) {
  const s = row.source;
  if (!s) return row.text;
  return [row.text, rmWords(s.rmMonth), `from Suggest machines${finite(s.suggestedAt) ? `, ${dayMonth(farmDate(s.suggestedAt))}` : ''}`].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
}

// A machine change in words, as the server names its task: "Fan 150 → 200 m³/h", "Dehumidifier none → 10 L/day".
export function changeText({ key, from, to }) {
  const [name, unit] = MACHINE_WORDS[key];
  const none = (v) => key === 'dehumidifierLday' && !(v > 0);
  const value = (v) => (none(v) ? 'none' : grouped(v));
  return none(to) ? `${name} ${value(from)} ${unit} → none` : `${name} ${value(from)} → ${value(to)} ${unit}`;
}

// The machines of a setup that differ from the room's now, in the order fan, aircon, humidifier, dehumidifier (0 = none).
export function machineChanges(now, next) {
  const v = (m, k) => (k === 'dehumidifierLday' ? (Number(m?.[k]) > 0 ? Number(m[k]) : 0) : Number(m?.[k]));
  return MACHINE_ORDER.filter((k) => Number.isFinite(v(now, k)) && Number.isFinite(v(next, k)) && v(now, k) !== v(next, k)).map((k) => {
    const change = { key: k, from: v(now, k), to: v(next, k) };
    return { ...change, text: changeText(change) };
  });
}

// Plan this: setup `index` of a suggestion against its baseline (the room's now). → { changes, rmMonth (null without an
// electricity price), rmText, body (for POST /api/rooms/:id/tasks) }, or null without a baseline (the "from" is unknown).
export function planOf(result, index, prices, suggestedAt) {
  const base = result?.baseline;
  const next = result?.best?.[index];
  if (!base?.machines || !next?.machines) return null;
  const runDays = finite(result.days) && result.days > 0 ? result.days : 1;
  const a = monthly(base, runDays, prices).rm;
  const b = monthly(next, runDays, prices).rm;
  const rmMonth = a === null || b === null ? null : Math.round((b - a) * 100) / 100;
  const changes = machineChanges(base.machines, next.machines);
  return {
    changes, rmMonth, rmText: rmWords(rmMonth),
    body: { suggestion: { machines: changes.map(({ key, from, to }) => ({ key, from, to })), rmMonth, suggestedAt } },
  };
}

// ---- Done on a long-term machine task (spec 7.5) ----

export const BTU_PER_W = 11.25; // the class rule Simulate uses between Setup's rated input and the aircon's BTU/h
export const acInputOf = (btu) => Math.round(btu / BTU_PER_W / 10) * 10;

const field = (name, label, unit, kind, before, after) => ({ name, label, unit, kind, before, after });

// The Setup fields a done machine task offers to update, before and after: the aircon's rated input, the humidifier's
// output, the dehumidifier fitted and its capacity (0 = none: not fitted). null: nothing on Setup (a fan: Setup keeps no fan
// flow).
export function setupUpdate(source, room) {
  if (!source || !room) return null;
  const to = Number(source.to);
  if (source.machine === 'acBTU') return [field('acInputW', 'Aircon rated input', 'W', 'number', room.acInputW ?? null, acInputOf(to))];
  if (source.machine === 'humidifierLH') return [field('humidifierLH', 'Humidifier output', 'L/h', 'number', room.humidifierLH ?? null, to)];
  if (source.machine === 'dehumidifierLday') {
    const fitted = room.dehumidifier === true;
    const box = field('dehumidifier', 'Dehumidifier fitted', null, 'check', fitted, to > 0);
    return to > 0 ? [box, field('dehumidifierLday', 'Dehumidifier capacity', 'L/day', 'number', fitted ? room.dehumidifierLday ?? null : null, to)] : [box];
  }
  return null;
}

export const beforeText = (f) => (f.kind === 'check' ? (f.before ? 'fitted' : 'not fitted') : finite(f.before) ? `${grouped(f.before)} ${f.unit}` : 'not set');

// Save and mark done: the room's PUT body with the dialog's texts over the room (texts: { name: text, or a box's
// true/false }), checked as Setup checks them. → { body } or { errors: { name: message } }
export function setupBody(room, texts) {
  const checked = parseRoomFields({ ...fieldTexts(room), ...texts }, Object.keys(texts));
  return checked.errors ? { errors: checked.errors } : { body: roomBody(room, checked.values) };
}

// ---- the dialogs' boxes ----

export const AMOUNT_MAX = 10_000;
export const REASON_MAX = 200;
export const TEXT_MAX = 120;
export const NOTE_MAX = 500;

// The amount box (empty: not weighed or counted) → { value } or { error }.
export function parseAmount(text, unit) {
  const s = String(text ?? '').trim();
  if (s === '') return { value: null };
  const n = /^[0-9]{1,5}(\.[0-9]{1,2})?$/.test(s) ? Number(s) : NaN;
  return n >= 0 && n <= AMOUNT_MAX ? { value: n } : { error: `Amount must be a number from 0 to 10,000${unit ? ` ${unit}` : ''}, or empty` };
}

// The reason of a skip or a cancel → { value } or { error }.
export function checkReason(text) {
  const s = String(text ?? '').trim();
  if (s === '') return { error: 'Say why' };
  return s.length > REASON_MAX ? { error: `At most ${REASON_MAX} characters` } : { value: s };
}

// Add a long-term task → { values: { text, note } } or { errors }.
export function checkLongterm({ text, note }) {
  const t = String(text ?? '').trim();
  const n = String(note ?? '').trim();
  const errors = {};
  if (t === '') errors.text = 'Say what the task is';
  else if (t.length > TEXT_MAX) errors.text = `At most ${TEXT_MAX} characters`;
  if (n.length > NOTE_MAX) errors.note = `At most ${NOTE_MAX} characters`;
  return Object.keys(errors).length ? { errors } : { values: { text: t, note: n || null } };
}

// ---- the Log ----

// A closed row as the Log shows it: { id, date, time, task, status, tone, worker, amount, reason }.
export function logLine(row) {
  const when = finite(row.closedAt) ? row.closedAt : row.createdAt;
  const at = finite(when) ? farmParts(when) : null;
  return {
    id: row.id, date: at ? dayMonth(at.date) : '', time: at ? at.time : '', task: row.text, status: STATUS_WORDS[row.status] ?? row.status,
    tone: row.status === 'done' ? 'good' : row.status === 'missed' ? 'amber' : 'none', worker: row.worker ?? '', amount: amountText(row), reason: row.reason ?? '',
  };
}

// The Log's lines: the closed rows of the last 14 days (every row read so far, after Older…).
export function logRows(rows, now, all = false) {
  const since = now - LOG_DAYS * DAY_MS;
  return (rows ?? []).filter((r) => all || (r.closedAt ?? r.createdAt ?? 0) >= since).map(logLine);
}
```

```js
// ---- the Settings task rules (spec 7.3) ----

const FAMILY_WORDS = { oyster: 'oyster', woodear: 'wood ear', reishi: 'reishi', milky: 'milky', lionsmane: "lion's mane" };
const capital = (s) => s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1);
const everyWords = (n) => (n === 1 ? 'every day' : `every ${n} days`);

// A daily rule's `when` in words.
export function whenWords(when) {
  if (when === 'daily') return 'every day';
  if (when === 'first') return 'first day of the stage';
  if (when === 'last') return 'last day of the stage';
  if (when && finite(when.every)) return `${everyWords(when.every)}${finite(when.lastDays) ? ` in the last ${when.lastDays} days of the stage` : ''}`;
  return '';
}

// A built-in rule's line under its text: "All crops · spawn run · every 2 days in the last 7 days of the stage · any time";
// an upkeep rule's "Every 7 days".
export function ruleLine(rule) {
  if (rule.kind === 'upkeep') return capital(everyWords(rule.everyDays));
  const out = rule.key === 'clearOut';
  const crops = out ? 'after the last flush' : rule.families?.length ? rule.families.map((f) => FAMILY_WORDS[f] ?? f).join(', ') : 'all crops';
  const stages = out ? null : rule.stages?.length ? rule.stages.map((s) => (STAGES[s] ?? s).toLowerCase()).join(', ') : 'all stages';
  return capital([crops, stages, whenWords(rule.when), SLOT_LABELS[rule.slot]?.toLowerCase()].filter(Boolean).join(' · '));
}

// An own rule's line: "Room 1, Room 2 · every 14 days · any time". rooms: GET /api/rooms, for the names.
export function customLine(rule, rooms = []) {
  const where = rule.rooms == null ? 'All rooms' : rule.rooms.map((id) => rooms.find((r) => r.id === id)?.name ?? `Room ${id}`).join(', ');
  return [where, rule.everyDays == null ? 'every day' : everyWords(rule.everyDays), SLOT_LABELS[rule.slot]?.toLowerCase()].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
}
```

```js
// ---- iteration 19: the Workers and Task rules cards (spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md 7.3) ----

export const WORKER_MAX = 40;
export const EVERY_MESSAGE = 'A whole number from 1 to 365 days';
const RULE_SLOTS = ['morning', 'afternoon', 'any'];

// A worker's name as typed → { value } or { error }. Taken: another active worker has it (in any case); a removed one may
// come back. selfId: the worker being renamed.
export function parseWorkerName(text, workers, selfId = null) {
  const s = String(text ?? '').trim();
  if (s === '') return { error: 'Type a name' };
  if (s.length > WORKER_MAX) return { error: `At most ${WORKER_MAX} characters` };
  const taken = (workers ?? []).some((w) => w.active && w.id !== selfId && w.name.toLowerCase() === s.toLowerCase());
  return taken ? { error: 'That name is taken' } : { value: s };
}

// The whole list for PUT /api/workers after one change: { add: name } (a removed worker of that name comes back),
// { rename: { id, name } }, { remove: id } (kept, inactive: the tasks it closed keep its name as text anyway).
export function workersAfter(workers, change) {
  const list = workers ?? [];
  if (change.add !== undefined) {
    const old = list.find((w) => !w.active && w.name.toLowerCase() === change.add.toLowerCase());
    return old ? list.map((w) => (w === old ? { ...w, name: change.add, active: true } : w)) : [...list, { name: change.add, active: true }];
  }
  if (change.rename) return list.map((w) => (w.id === change.rename.id ? { ...w, name: change.rename.name } : w));
  if (change.remove !== undefined) return list.map((w) => (w.id === change.remove ? { ...w, active: false } : w));
  return list;
}

// An upkeep rule's interval → { value } (whole days, 1 to 365) or { error }.
export function parseEveryDays(text) {
  const s = String(text ?? '').trim();
  if (!/^[0-9]{1,3}$/.test(s)) return { error: EVERY_MESSAGE };
  const n = Number(s);
  return n >= 1 && n <= 365 ? { value: n } : { error: EVERY_MESSAGE };
}

// The Add your own form → { value } for the custom list of PUT /api/task-rules, or { errors: { field: message } }. often:
// 'daily' (every day) or 'every' (every so many days from the last time done: an upkeep rule).
export function parseCustomRule({ text, allRooms, rooms, often, everyDays, slot }) {
  const errors = {};
  const s = String(text ?? '').trim();
  if (s === '') errors.text = 'Say what the task is';
  else if (s.length > 120) errors.text = 'At most 120 characters';
  if (!allRooms && !(rooms?.length > 0)) errors.rooms = 'Pick at least one room';
  const every = often === 'every' ? parseEveryDays(everyDays) : { value: null };
  if (every.error) errors.everyDays = every.error;
  if (!RULE_SLOTS.includes(slot)) errors.slot = 'Pick a time of day';
  if (Object.keys(errors).length) return { errors };
  return { value: { text: s, rooms: allRooms ? null : [...rooms], everyDays: every.value, slot, enabled: true } };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `web/task-dialogs.js`**

```js
// The dialogs of the Tasks tab, the Today page and Plan this (spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md 7.1, 7.2, 7.4 and 7.5): who
// did it (a name from Settings' workers, kept in this browser), Done with an amount, Skip and Cancel with a reason, Add a
// long-term task, Done on a long-term machine task with the Setup update, and Plan this. One dialog, made on first use and
// shared, like the Simulate stepper.
//   createTaskActions({ onChanged(row | null), onError(text) }) → { done(row), skip(row), cancel(row), add(roomId), destroy() }
//   createWorkerPicker() → { node, destroy() } · openPlanDialog({ roomId, roomName, rank, plan, onPlanned(rows) })
//   getWorker() · setWorker(name) · loadWorkers() → names · closeTaskDialog()

import { request, OFFLINE } from './api.js';
import { keepLines } from './text-math.js';
import { recordsAmount, amountField, parseAmount, checkReason, checkLongterm, setupUpdate, beforeText, setupBody } from './tasks-math.js';

const WORKER_KEY = 'farm.worker';
const COMMAND_TIMEOUT = 15000;

let dialog = null;
let openSeq = 0; // every opening gets a token; an answer that finds it changed is dropped

function el(tag, className, content) {
  const node = document.createElement(tag);
  if (className) node.className = className;
  if (content !== undefined) node.textContent = content;
  return node;
}

function button(label, className) {
  const b = el('button', className ?? '', label);
  b.type = 'button';
  return b;
}

const errorText = (err) => (err.status === 0 ? `${OFFLINE}. Try again.` : err.message);

// ---- who is doing the rounds ----

export function getWorker() {
  try { return localStorage.getItem(WORKER_KEY) || null; } catch { return null; }
}

export function setWorker(name) {
  try {
    if (name) localStorage.setItem(WORKER_KEY, name);
    else localStorage.removeItem(WORKER_KEY);
  } catch { /* not kept: the next Done asks again */ }
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('farm:worker'));
}

// The active workers' names (GET /api/workers).
export async function loadWorkers() {
  const data = await request('GET', '/api/workers');
  return (Array.isArray(data?.workers) ? data.workers : []).filter((w) => w.active).map((w) => w.name);
}

// "Doing the rounds as: Ali ▾" (spec 7.2): the name kept in this browser; another picker or a dialog changing it repaints it.
export function createWorkerPicker() {
  let alive = true;
  let names = [];
  const node = el('label', 'tk-as');
  const select = el('select');
  select.name = 'worker';
  node.append(el('span', '', 'Doing the rounds as'), select);
  function paint() {
    const now = getWorker();
    const chosen = names.includes(now) ? now : '';
    select.replaceChildren(...[['', names.length ? 'Pick a name' : 'No names yet (Settings)'], ...names.map((n) => [n, n])].map(([value, label]) => {
      const o = el('option', '', label);
      o.value = value;
      o.selected = value === chosen;
      return o;
    }));
  }
  select.addEventListener('change', () => setWorker(select.value || null));
  const onStore = () => { if (alive) paint(); };
  window.addEventListener('farm:worker', onStore);
  loadWorkers().then((list) => { if (alive) { names = list; paint(); } }).catch(() => { /* the dialogs ask again */ });
  paint();
  return {
    node,
    destroy() {
      alive = false;
      window.removeEventListener('farm:worker', onStore);
    },
  };
}

// ---- the dialog ----

function ensureDialog() {
  if (dialog) return dialog;
  dialog = el('dialog', 'tk-dialog');
  dialog.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'tk-dialog-title');
  document.body.append(dialog);
  return dialog;
}

export function closeTaskDialog() {
  openSeq++;
  if (dialog?.open) dialog.close();
}

// Opens the dialog on a form. lead: lines (text or nodes) under the title; fields: [{ name, label, kind: 'text' | 'number'
// | 'area' | 'select' | 'check', value, options ([value, label]), hint }]; buttons: [{ value, label, primary }] after the
// close button (closeLabel; Close when there are no buttons). submit(values, which) → true (closes), or { field, message }
// or { message } to show while it stays open.
function openForm({ title, lead = [], fields = [], buttons = [], closeLabel = buttons.length ? 'Cancel' : 'Close', submit }) {
  const d = ensureDialog();
  if (d.open) d.close();
  const me = ++openSeq;
  const alive = () => me === openSeq;
  d.replaceChildren();
  const form = el('form');
  form.noValidate = true;
  const heading = el('h2', '', title);
  heading.id = 'tk-dialog-title';
  heading.tabIndex = -1;
  const formError = el('p', 'form-error');
  formError.setAttribute('role', 'alert');
  formError.tabIndex = -1;
  formError.hidden = true;
  form.append(heading, ...lead.map((x) => (typeof x === 'string' ? el('p', 'tk-lead', keepLines(x)) : x)), formError);
  const inputs = new Map();
  const errors = new Map();
  for (const f of fields) {
    const id = `tk-f-${f.name}`;
    const wrap = el('div', f.kind === 'check' ? 'field tk-check' : 'field');
    let input;
    if (f.kind === 'select') {
      input = el('select');
      for (const [value, label] of f.options) {
        const o = el('option', '', label);
        o.value = value;
        o.selected = value === f.value;
        input.append(o);
      }
    } else if (f.kind === 'area') {
      input = el('textarea');
      input.rows = 3;
      input.value = f.value ?? '';
    } else if (f.kind === 'check') {
      input = el('input');
      input.type = 'checkbox';
      input.checked = f.value === true;
    } else {
      input = el('input');
      input.type = 'text';
      input.autocomplete = 'off';
      if (f.kind === 'number') input.inputMode = 'decimal';
      input.value = f.value ?? '';
    }
    input.id = id;
    input.name = f.name;
    const label = el('label', '', f.label);
    label.htmlFor = id;
    const err = el('p', 'field-error');
    err.id = `${id}-error`;
    err.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');
    const described = [err.id];
    wrap.append(...(f.kind === 'check' ? [input, label] : [label, input]));
    if (f.hint) {
      const hint = el('p', 'c-hint', keepLines(f.hint));
      hint.id = `${id}-hint`;
      described.unshift(hint.id);
      wrap.append(hint);
    }
    wrap.append(err);
    input.setAttribute('aria-describedby', described.join(' '));
    input.addEventListener('input', () => { err.textContent = ''; input.removeAttribute('aria-invalid'); });
    inputs.set(f.name, input);
    errors.set(f.name, err);
    form.append(wrap);
  }
  const actions = el('div', 'actions');
  const close = button(closeLabel);
  const sends = buttons.map((b) => {
    const x = button(b.label, b.primary ? 'primary' : '');
    if (b.primary) x.type = 'submit';
    x.value = b.value;
    return x;
  });
  actions.append(close, ...sends);
  form.append(actions);
  d.append(form);

  let busy = false;
  const values = () => Object.fromEntries([...inputs].map(([name, input]) => [name, input.type === 'checkbox' ? input.checked : input.value]));
  function show(problem) {
    const input = problem.field ? inputs.get(problem.field) : null;
    if (input) {
      errors.get(problem.field).textContent = problem.message;
      input.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
      input.focus();
      return;
    }
    formError.textContent = problem.message;
    formError.hidden = false;
    formError.focus();
  }
  async function run(which) {
    if (busy) return;
    busy = true;
    formError.hidden = true;
    for (const [name, err] of errors) { err.textContent = ''; inputs.get(name).removeAttribute('aria-invalid'); }
    for (const b of [close, ...sends]) b.disabled = true;
    let outcome;
    try {
      outcome = await submit(values(), which);
    } catch (err) {
      outcome = { message: errorText(err) };
    }
    if (!alive()) return;
    busy = false;
    for (const b of [close, ...sends]) b.disabled = false;
    if (outcome === true) d.close();
    else show(outcome);
  }
  for (const b of sends) if (b.type !== 'submit') b.addEventListener('click', () => run(b.value));
  form.addEventListener('submit', (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const main = buttons.find((b) => b.primary) ?? buttons[0];
    if (main) run(main.value);
  });
  close.addEventListener('click', () => d.close());
  d.showModal();
  (fields.length ? inputs.get(fields[0].name) : heading).focus();
}

// ---- Done, Skip, Cancel, Add ----

export function createTaskActions({ onChanged, onError }) {
  let alive = true;
  let names = null; // the workers' names, read on the first need

  async function workers() {
    if (names) return names;
    try { names = await loadWorkers(); } catch { names = []; }
    return names;
  }
  const workerField = (list) => ({
    name: 'worker', label: 'Who did it', kind: 'select', value: list.includes(getWorker()) ? getWorker() : '',
    options: [['', 'Pick a name'], ...list.map((n) => [n, n])],
  });
  function settingsLine() {
    const p = el('p', 'tk-lead');
    const a = el('a', '', 'Settings');
    a.href = '#/settings';
    p.append("No names yet: add the workers' names in ", a, ' first.');
    return p;
  }
  const leadOf = (row, list, more = []) => [row.text, ...more, ...(list.length ? [] : [settingsLine()])];

  // The worker picked in a dialog is kept for the next Done (spec 7.2).
  function picked(values) {
    const w = String(values.worker ?? '').trim();
    if (w) setWorker(w);
    return w || null;
  }

  // A command. After it the list is asked again, and after a 409 or 404 too (someone else closed it, or it is gone).
  async function send(path, body) {
    try {
      const row = await request('POST', path, body, COMMAND_TIMEOUT);
      if (alive) onChanged?.(row);
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      if (alive && (err.status === 409 || err.status === 404)) onChanged?.(null);
      return err.status === 400 && err.field ? { field: err.field, message: err.message } : { message: errorText(err) };
    }
  }

  async function done(row) {
    if (row.kind === 'longterm' && row.source) return machineDone(row);
    const list = await workers();
    if (!alive) return;
    const worker = getWorker();
    // One tap: a stage or upkeep task with no amount, by the worker this browser does the rounds as.
    if (row.kind !== 'longterm' && !recordsAmount(row) && worker && list.includes(worker)) {
      const r = await send(`/api/tasks/${row.id}/done`, { worker });
      if (r !== true && alive) onError?.(r.message);
      return;
    }
    const amount = recordsAmount(row) ? amountField(row) : null;
    openForm({
      title: 'Mark done',
      lead: leadOf(row, list),
      fields: [workerField(list), ...(amount ? [{ name: 'amount', label: amount.label, kind: 'number', value: '', hint: 'Leave it empty if it was not weighed or counted.' }] : [])],
      buttons: [{ value: 'done', label: 'Done', primary: true }],
      submit: async (v) => {
        const w = picked(v);
        if (!w) return { field: 'worker', message: 'Pick who did it' };
        let a = null;
        if (amount) {
          const p = parseAmount(v.amount, amount.unit);
          if (p.error) return { field: 'amount', message: p.error };
          a = p.value;
        }
        return send(`/api/tasks/${row.id}/done`, { worker: w, ...(a === null ? {} : { amount: a }) });
      },
    });
  }

  async function skip(row) {
    const list = await workers();
    if (!alive) return;
    openForm({
      title: 'Skip this task',
      lead: leadOf(row, list),
      fields: [workerField(list), { name: 'reason', label: 'Why', kind: 'text', value: '' }],
      buttons: [{ value: 'skip', label: 'Skip', primary: true }],
      submit: async (v) => {
        const w = picked(v);
        if (!w) return { field: 'worker', message: 'Pick who did it' };
        const r = checkReason(v.reason);
        if (r.error) return { field: 'reason', message: r.error };
        return send(`/api/tasks/${row.id}/skip`, { worker: w, reason: r.value });
      },
    });
  }

  function cancel(row) {
    openForm({
      title: 'Cancel this task',
      lead: [row.text, 'It stays in the log as cancelled, with the reason.'],
      fields: [{ name: 'reason', label: 'Why', kind: 'text', value: '' }],
      buttons: [{ value: 'cancel', label: 'Cancel the task', primary: true }],
      closeLabel: 'Keep the task',
      submit: async (v) => {
        const r = checkReason(v.reason);
        if (r.error) return { field: 'reason', message: r.error };
        return send(`/api/tasks/${row.id}/cancel`, { reason: r.value });
      },
    });
  }

  function add(roomId) {
    openForm({
      title: 'Add a long-term task',
      lead: ['A job of weeks rather than of the day, such as fixing the door seal.'],
      fields: [{ name: 'text', label: 'Task', kind: 'text', value: '' }, { name: 'note', label: 'Note (optional)', kind: 'area', value: '' }],
      buttons: [{ value: 'add', label: 'Add', primary: true }],
      submit: async (v) => {
        const c = checkLongterm(v);
        if (c.errors) {
          const [field] = Object.keys(c.errors);
          return { field, message: c.errors[field] };
        }
        try {
          const data = await request('POST', `/api/rooms/${roomId}/tasks`, { text: c.values.text, ...(c.values.note ? { note: c.values.note } : {}) }, COMMAND_TIMEOUT);
          if (alive) onChanged?.(data?.rows?.[0] ?? null);
          return true;
        } catch (err) {
          return err.status === 400 && err.field ? { field: err.field, message: err.message } : { message: errorText(err) };
        }
      },
    });
  }

  // Done on a long-term machine task (spec 7.5): the Setup values before and after, editable; Save and mark done saves Setup
  // first (the room's PUT) and marks the task done only when that worked; Just mark done leaves Setup as it is.
  async function machineDone(row) {
    const list = await workers();
    let room;
    try {
      room = await request('GET', `/api/rooms/${row.roomId}`);
    } catch (err) {
      if (alive) onError?.(errorText(err));
      return;
    }
    if (!alive) return;
    const update = setupUpdate(row.source, room);
    const mark = async (v) => {
      const w = picked(v);
      if (!w) return { field: 'worker', message: 'Pick who did it' };
      return send(`/api/tasks/${row.id}/done`, { worker: w });
    };
    if (!update) {
      openForm({
        title: 'Mark done', lead: leadOf(row, list, ['Setup keeps no fan flow, so Done only records the change.']),
        fields: [workerField(list)], buttons: [{ value: 'done', label: 'Done', primary: true }], submit: mark,
      });
      return;
    }
    let saved = false; // Setup was saved in this dialog: a second try only marks the task done
    openForm({
      title: 'Mark done and update Setup',
      lead: leadOf(row, list, [`Setup now: ${update.map((f) => `${f.label.toLowerCase()} ${beforeText(f)}`).join(' · ')}`]),
      fields: [workerField(list), ...update.map((f) => (f.kind === 'check'
        ? { name: f.name, label: f.label, kind: 'check', value: f.after }
        : { name: f.name, label: `${f.label} (${f.unit})`, kind: 'number', value: String(f.after), hint: `Now ${beforeText(f)}` }))],
      buttons: [{ value: 'just', label: 'Just mark done' }, { value: 'save', label: 'Save and mark done', primary: true }],
      submit: async (v, which) => {
        if (!String(v.worker ?? '').trim()) return { field: 'worker', message: 'Pick who did it' };
        if (which === 'save' && !saved) {
          const built = setupBody(room, Object.fromEntries(update.map((f) => [f.name, v[f.name]])));
          if (built.errors) {
            const [field] = Object.keys(built.errors);
            return { field, message: built.errors[field] };
          }
          try {
            room = await request('PUT', `/api/rooms/${row.roomId}`, built.body, COMMAND_TIMEOUT);
            saved = true;
          } catch (err) {
            return { field: err.status === 400 ? err.field : undefined, message: `Setup not saved: ${errorText(err)}` };
          }
        }
        const r = await mark(v);
        return r !== true && saved ? { message: `Setup saved. Marking the task done failed: ${r.message}` } : r;
      },
    });
  }

  return {
    done, skip, cancel, add,
    destroy() {
      alive = false;
      closeTaskDialog();
    },
  };
}

// ---- Plan this (spec 7.4) ----

// Each machine that differs from what the room has now (from → to) and the RM a month against it; Plan posts them as the
// room's long-term tasks. plan: tasks-math planOf's. onPlanned(rows) after it worked.
export function openPlanDialog({ roomId, roomName, rank, plan, onPlanned }) {
  const changes = el('ul', 'tk-changes');
  changes.append(...plan.changes.map((c) => el('li', '', keepLines(c.text))));
  const lead = plan.changes.length
    ? [`Against what ${roomName} has now${plan.rmText ? ` · ${plan.rmText}` : ''}:`, changes,
      "Each change becomes a long-term task on the room's Tasks tab; Done on one offers to update Setup."]
    : ['Nothing changes from what the room has now.'];
  openForm({
    title: `Plan setup ${rank}`,
    lead,
    buttons: plan.changes.length ? [{ value: 'plan', label: 'Plan', primary: true }] : [],
    submit: async () => {
      try {
        const data = await request('POST', `/api/rooms/${roomId}/tasks`, plan.body, COMMAND_TIMEOUT);
        onPlanned?.(Array.isArray(data?.rows) ? data.rows : []);
        return true;
      } catch (err) {
        return { message: errorText(err) };
      }
    },
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `web/task-list.js`**

```js
// The task rows shared by the room's Tasks tab and the Today page (spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md 7.1 and 7.2): a row's
// text with the crop notes muted under it and its due words, Done and Skip (or Cancel) in place; a slot's closed rows
// folded under "6 done". actions: createTaskActions' { done, skip, cancel }.

import { closedLine } from './tasks-math.js';
import { keepLines } from './text-math.js';

export function el(tag, className, content) {
  const node = document.createElement(tag);
  if (className) node.className = className;
  if (content !== undefined) node.textContent = content;
  return node;
}

export function button(label, className) {
  const b = el('button', className ?? '', label);
  b.type = 'button';
  return b;
}

// One task as a list item. due: dueWords' { text, tone } (upkeep); line: the first line (a long-term row's longtermLine);
// skip, cancel: the second button of an open row.
export function taskRow(row, actions, { due = null, line = row.text, skip = true, cancel = false } = {}) {
  const li = el('li', 'tk-row');
  li.dataset.status = row.status;
  li.dataset.kind = row.kind;
  const text = el('div', 'tk-text');
  text.append(el('p', 'tk-title', keepLines(line)));
  if (row.note) text.append(el('p', 'tk-note', keepLines(row.note)));
  if (due?.text) {
    const words = el('p', 'tk-due', due.text);
    if (due.tone) words.dataset.tone = due.tone;
    text.append(words);
  }
  li.append(text);
  if (row.status !== 'open') {
    text.append(el('p', 'tk-closed-line', keepLines(closedLine(row))));
    return li;
  }
  const box = el('div', 'tk-actions');
  const done = button('Done');
  done.setAttribute('aria-label', `Done: ${row.text}`);
  done.dataset.focusKey = `done-${row.id}`;
  done.addEventListener('click', () => actions.done(row));
  box.append(done);
  if (skip) {
    const b = button('Skip');
    b.setAttribute('aria-label', `Skip: ${row.text}`);
    b.dataset.focusKey = `skip-${row.id}`;
    b.addEventListener('click', () => actions.skip(row));
    box.append(b);
  }
  if (cancel) {
    const b = button('Cancel');
    b.setAttribute('aria-label', `Cancel the task: ${row.text}`);
    b.dataset.focusKey = `cancel-${row.id}`;
    b.addEventListener('click', () => actions.cancel(row));
    box.append(b);
  }
  li.append(box);
  return li;
}

// One slot of the day (slotGroups' group): its title, its open rows, and its closed rows folded under "6 done". openFolds:
// the folds a repaint keeps open (by foldKey).
export function slotPart(group, actions, { level = 'h3', openFolds = new Set(), foldKey = group.slot } = {}) {
  const part = el('div', 'tk-slot');
  part.append(el(level, 'tk-slot-title', group.label));
  if (group.open.length) {
    const list = el('ul', 'tk-list');
    list.append(...group.open.map((row) => taskRow(row, actions)));
    part.append(list);
  }
  if (group.closed.length) {
    const fold = el('details', 'tk-fold');
    fold.open = openFolds.has(foldKey);
    fold.addEventListener('toggle', () => { if (fold.open) openFolds.add(foldKey); else openFolds.delete(foldKey); });
    const list = el('ul', 'tk-list');
    list.append(...group.closed.map((row) => taskRow(row, actions)));
    fold.append(el('summary', '', group.fold), list);
    part.append(fold);
  }
  return part;
}

// Keeps the focus on the same button across a repaint (a refresh must not throw it to the top of the page).
export function keepFocus(host, paint) {
  const key = host.contains(document.activeElement) ? document.activeElement.dataset?.focusKey : null;
  paint();
  if (key) host.querySelector(`[data-focus-key="${key}"]`)?.focus();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `web/tasks-card.js`**

```js
// The room's Tasks tab (spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md 7.1): Today (the day's stage tasks by Morning, Afternoon and Any
// time, the closed ones folded), Upkeep (overdue first), Long-term (Plan this on Suggest machines, or Add) and the Log of the
// last 14 days with Older… and Export CSV. createTasksCard(host, { roomId, onCounts, onGone }) → { refresh(), destroy() };
// onCounts(counts | null) gives the tab its dot. The card asks for its own data: on the room's tasks events, when the stream
// comes back, and on a timer (30 s; 60 s while the stream is up: the day turns over at midnight without an event).

import { request, OUTDATED, routeMissing } from './api.js';
import { live } from './live.js';
import { fallbackMs, createThrottle } from './live-math.js';
import { slotGroups, upkeepRows, longtermLine, logRows, dayTitle, tasksDueWords, LOG_DAYS, LOG_PAGE } from './tasks-math.js';
import { createTaskActions, createWorkerPicker } from './task-dialogs.js';
import { el, button, taskRow, slotPart, keepFocus } from './task-list.js';
import { keepLines, DOT } from './text-math.js';

const REFRESH_MS = 30_000;

export function createTasksCard(host, { roomId, onCounts, onGone }) {
  let alive = true;
  let seq = 0; // every load takes a token; an answer that finds it changed is dropped
  let logSeq = 0;
  let timer = null;
  let view = null; // GET /api/rooms/:id/tasks
  let painted = '';
  let log = []; // the closed rows read so far, newest first
  let logMore = false; // the last page was full: there may be older ones
  let logAll = false; // Older… was pressed: every row read so far, not only the last 14 days
  let logFailed = false;
  const openFolds = new Set();

  host.className = 'tk-grid';

  // Today
  const todayCard = el('section', 'card tk-part tk-today');
  const todayHead = el('div', 'sim-card-head');
  const picker = createWorkerPicker();
  todayHead.append(el('h2', '', 'Today'), picker.node);
  const todaySub = el('p', 'muted tk-sub', 'Loading…');
  const error = el('p', 'meta warn tk-error');
  error.setAttribute('role', 'alert');
  error.hidden = true;
  const todayBody = el('div', 'tk-body');
  todayCard.append(todayHead, todaySub, error, todayBody);

  // Upkeep
  const upkeepCard = el('section', 'card tk-part');
  const upkeepList = el('ul', 'tk-list');
  const upkeepNone = el('p', 'none', 'No upkeep for this room.');
  upkeepNone.hidden = true;
  upkeepCard.append(el('h2', '', 'Upkeep'), el('p', 'muted tk-sub', 'Each counted from the last time it was done.'), upkeepList, upkeepNone);

  // Long-term
  const longCard = el('section', 'card tk-part');
  const longHead = el('div', 'sim-card-head');
  const add = button('Add');
  add.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Add a long-term task');
  longHead.append(el('h2', '', 'Long-term'), add);
  const longList = el('ul', 'tk-list');
  const longNone = el('p', 'none', 'None open. Plan this on Suggest machines, or Add your own.');
  longNone.hidden = true;
  longCard.append(longHead, longList, longNone);

  // Log
  const logCard = el('section', 'card tk-part');
  const logHead = el('div', 'sim-card-head');
  const csv = el('a', 'button', 'Export CSV');
  csv.href = `/api/rooms/${roomId}/tasks/log.csv`;
  csv.setAttribute('download', '');
  logHead.append(el('h2', '', 'Log'), csv);
  const logList = el('ul', 'tk-log');
  const logNone = el('p', 'none');
  logNone.hidden = true;
  const older = button('Older…');
  older.hidden = true;
  logCard.append(logHead, el('p', 'muted tk-sub', `Done, skipped, missed and cancelled tasks of the last ${LOG_DAYS} days. Export CSV has them all, for the myGAP records.`),
    logList, logNone, older);

  host.replaceChildren(todayCard, upkeepCard, longCard, logCard);

  const actions = createTaskActions({
    onChanged: () => { if (alive) { error.hidden = true; refresh(); loadLog(); } },
    onError: (text) => { if (alive) { error.textContent = text; error.hidden = false; } },
  });

  function paint() {
    const key = JSON.stringify(view);
    if (key === painted) return;
    painted = key;
    keepFocus(host, () => {
      todaySub.textContent = `${dayTitle(view.date)}${DOT}${tasksDueWords(view.counts?.due ?? 0)}`;
      const groups = slotGroups(view.today);
      todayBody.replaceChildren(...(groups.length ? groups.map((g) => slotPart(g, actions, { openFolds })) : [el('p', 'none', 'No stage tasks today.')]));
      const up = upkeepRows(view.upkeep, view.date);
      upkeepList.replaceChildren(...up.map(({ row, due }) => taskRow(row, actions, { due })));
      upkeepNone.hidden = up.length > 0;
      const open = (view.longterm ?? []).filter((r) => r.status === 'open');
      longList.replaceChildren(...open.map((row) => taskRow(row, actions, { line: longtermLine(row), skip: false, cancel: true })));
      longNone.hidden = open.length > 0;
    });
    onCounts?.(view.counts ?? null);
  }

  async function refresh() {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    const me = ++seq;
    try {
      const data = await request('GET', `/api/rooms/${roomId}/tasks`);
      if (!alive || me !== seq) return;
      view = data;
      paint();
    } catch (err) {
      if (!alive || me !== seq) return;
      if (routeMissing(err)) { // a farm app from before the tasks: say so, no dot, no empty parts, no timer
        todaySub.textContent = OUTDATED;
        for (const node of [picker.node, upkeepCard, longCard, logCard]) node.hidden = true;
        onCounts?.(null);
        return;
      }
      if (err.status === 404) { onGone?.(); return; }
      if (!view) todaySub.textContent = "Couldn't load the tasks";
    }
    if (alive) timer = setTimeout(refresh, fallbackMs(REFRESH_MS, live.connected()));
  }

  // The Log: the first page again (after a change), or the next older page (Older…).
  async function loadLog(next = false) {
    const me = ++logSeq;
    const before = next && log.length ? log[log.length - 1].id : null;
    try {
      const data = await request('GET', `/api/rooms/${roomId}/tasks/log${before === null ? '' : `?before=${before}`}`);
      if (!alive || me !== logSeq) return;
      const rows = Array.isArray(data?.rows) ? data.rows : [];
      log = before === null ? rows : [...log, ...rows];
      logMore = rows.length >= LOG_PAGE;
      logFailed = false;
    } catch {
      if (!alive || me !== logSeq) return;
      logFailed = true;
    }
    paintLog();
  }

  function paintLog() {
    const lines = logRows(log, Date.now(), logAll);
    logList.replaceChildren(...lines.map((l) => {
      const li = el('li');
      const head = el('p', 'tk-log-head');
      const status = el('span', 'tk-log-status', l.status);
      status.dataset.tone = l.tone;
      head.append(`${l.date} ${l.time}${DOT}`, status, ...[l.worker, l.amount].filter(Boolean).map((t) => `${DOT}${t}`));
      li.append(head, el('p', 'tk-log-task', keepLines(l.task)));
      if (l.reason) li.append(el('p', 'tk-log-reason', keepLines(l.reason)));
      return li;
    }));
    logNone.textContent = logFailed ? "Couldn't load the log" : `Nothing closed in the last ${LOG_DAYS} days.`;
    logNone.hidden = lines.length > 0 && !logFailed;
    older.hidden = !(logMore || (!logAll && lines.length < log.length));
  }

  older.addEventListener('click', () => {
    logAll = true;
    if (logMore) loadLog(true);
    else paintLog();
  });
  add.addEventListener('click', () => actions.add(roomId));

  // The room's tasks moved (a Done elsewhere, a new day made, a stage change): ask again, at most once in 2 s.
  const reload = createThrottle(() => { if (alive) { refresh(); loadLog(); } });
  const offLive = [
    live.on('room', (d) => { if (d.id === roomId && d.part === 'tasks') reload.call(); }),
    live.onStatus((up) => { if (up) reload.call(); }),
  ];
  refresh();
  loadLog();

  return {
    refresh,
    destroy() {
      alive = false;
      seq++;
      logSeq++;
      clearTimeout(timer);
      reload.cancel();
      for (const off of offLive) off();
      actions.destroy();
      picker.destroy();
      host.replaceChildren();
    },
  };
}
```

```js
  assert.match(page, /import \{ tabDot \} from '\.\/tasks-math\.js';/);
```

```js
  // The tab bar: a link per tab (the router calls setTab when the address changes); the current one marked. The Tasks tab
  // carries the number of tasks due (spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md 7.1).
  const tabBar = q('[data-tabs]');
  let tasksLink = null;
  let tasksDot = null;
  for (const t of ROOM_TABS) {
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = roomHash(id, t.key);
    a.dataset.tab = t.key;
    a.append(text('span', '', t.label));
    if (t.key === 'tasks') {
      tasksDot = text('span', 'tab-dot tab-count');
      tasksDot.hidden = true;
      tasksDot.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true'); // the link's name says it
      a.append(tasksDot);
      tasksLink = a;
    }
    tabBar.append(a);
  }
  // The Tasks tab's dot: the stage tasks open today and the upkeep due or overdue (amber with an overdue one).
  function paintTasksDot(counts) {
    const dot = tabDot(counts);
    tasksDot.hidden = !dot;
    if (!dot) {
      tasksLink.removeAttribute('aria-label');
      return;
    }
    tasksDot.textContent = dot.text;
    tasksDot.dataset.level = dot.level;
    tasksLink.setAttribute('aria-label', `Tasks: ${dot.label}`);
  }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `web/today-page.js`**

```js
// The farm's Today page, #/today (spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md 7.2): who is doing the rounds (a name from Settings, kept
// in this browser), every room in the Overview's order with its open stage tasks by slot and its overdue upkeep, Done and
// Skip in place, the closed ones folded under "6 done", and a line per room with open long-term tasks to its Tasks tab.

import { request, OFFLINE, OUTDATED, routeMissing } from './api.js';
import { roomHash } from './route.js';
import { live } from './live.js';
import { fallbackMs, createThrottle } from './live-math.js';
import { slotGroups, upkeepRows, dayTitle, tasksDueWords, longtermWords, todayCount } from './tasks-math.js';
import { createTaskActions, createWorkerPicker } from './task-dialogs.js';
import { el, taskRow, slotPart, keepFocus } from './task-list.js';
import { DOT } from './text-math.js';

const REFRESH_MS = 30_000;

const TEMPLATE = `
  <div class="page-head">
    <div class="page-titles">
      <h1 class="page-title" tabindex="-1">Today</h1>
      <p class="page-sub" data-sub>Loading…</p>
    </div>
    <div class="td-picker" data-picker></div>
  </div>
  <div class="banner" role="alert" hidden>
    <span data-banner-text></span>
    <button type="button" data-retry>Retry</button>
  </div>
  <p class="meta warn tk-error" data-error role="alert" hidden></p>
  <div class="td-rooms" data-rooms></div>
  <section class="card td-long" data-long hidden>
    <h2>Long-term</h2>
    <ul class="td-long-list" data-long-list></ul>
  </section>`;

let mountSeq = 0; // every mount gets a token; an answer that finds the token changed is dropped

export function mount(container) {
  const me = ++mountSeq;
  const alive = () => me === mountSeq;
  container.innerHTML = TEMPLATE;
  const q = (sel) => container.querySelector(sel);
  const els = {
    sub: q('[data-sub]'), banner: q('.banner'), bannerText: q('[data-banner-text]'), error: q('[data-error]'), rooms: q('[data-rooms]'),
    long: q('[data-long]'), longList: q('[data-long-list]'),
  };
  document.title = 'Today · Mushroom farm';
  const picker = createWorkerPicker();
  q('[data-picker]').append(picker.node);

  let view = null; // GET /api/tasks/today
  let seq = 0;
  let timer = null;
  let painted = '';
  const openFolds = new Set();

  const actions = createTaskActions({
    onChanged: () => { if (alive()) { els.error.hidden = true; load(); } },
    onError: (text) => { if (alive()) { els.error.textContent = text; els.error.hidden = false; } },
  });

  // A room: its name (to its Tasks tab), its open stage tasks by slot (the closed ones folded) and its upkeep due.
  function roomPart(r) {
    const card = el('section', 'card td-room');
    const title = el('h2');
    const link = el('a', '', r.name);
    link.href = roomHash(r.id, 'tasks');
    title.append(link);
    card.append(title);
    const groups = slotGroups(r.today);
    for (const g of groups) card.append(slotPart(g, actions, { openFolds, foldKey: `${r.id}:${g.slot}` }));
    const up = upkeepRows(r.upkeepDue, view.date);
    if (up.length) {
      const part = el('div', 'tk-slot');
      const list = el('ul', 'tk-list');
      list.append(...up.map(({ row, due }) => taskRow(row, actions, { due })));
      part.append(el('h3', 'tk-slot-title', 'Upkeep'), list);
      card.append(part);
    }
    if (!groups.some((g) => g.open.length) && up.length === 0) card.append(el('p', 'none', groups.length ? 'All done for today.' : 'Nothing due today.'));
    return card;
  }

  function paint() {
    const key = JSON.stringify(view);
    if (key === painted) return;
    painted = key;
    keepFocus(container, () => {
      els.sub.textContent = `${dayTitle(view.date)}${DOT}${tasksDueWords(todayCount(view) ?? 0)}`;
      els.rooms.replaceChildren(...(view.rooms.length ? view.rooms.map(roomPart) : [el('p', 'none', 'No rooms yet. Add one on the Overview.')]));
      const long = view.rooms.filter((r) => r.longterm > 0);
      els.long.hidden = long.length === 0;
      els.longList.replaceChildren(...long.map((r) => {
        const li = el('li');
        const a = el('a', '', `${r.name}: ${longtermWords(r.longterm)}`);
        a.href = roomHash(r.id, 'tasks');
        li.append(a);
        return li;
      }));
    });
  }

  async function load() {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    const mine = ++seq;
    try {
      const data = await request('GET', '/api/tasks/today');
      if (!alive() || mine !== seq) return;
      view = data;
      els.banner.hidden = true;
      paint();
    } catch (err) {
      if (!alive() || mine !== seq) return;
      els.bannerText.textContent = routeMissing(err) ? OUTDATED : err.status === 0 ? OFFLINE : err.message;
      els.banner.hidden = false;
      if (!view) els.sub.textContent = '';
    }
    if (alive()) timer = setTimeout(load, fallbackMs(REFRESH_MS, live.connected()));
  }

  q('[data-retry]').addEventListener('click', load);
  // Any room's tasks moved (the server sends the tasks event after every change): ask again, at most once in 2 s.
  const reload = createThrottle(() => { if (alive()) load(); });
  const offLive = [
    live.on('tasks', () => reload.call()),
    live.onStatus((up) => { if (up) reload.call(); }),
  ];
  load();

  return function unmount() {
    mountSeq++;
    clearTimeout(timer);
    reload.cancel();
    for (const off of offLive) off();
    actions.destroy();
    picker.destroy();
    document.title = 'Rooms · Mushroom farm';
    container.replaceChildren();
  };
}
```

(e) After line 146 (the end of `live.on('alarms', …);`) add:

```js

// The Today link's count (spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md 7.2): the stage and upkeep tasks due today in every room, asked
// with the alarm badge and pushed by the live stream's tasks event; hidden at 0, and on a farm app without tasks (404).
let todayDue = 0;
let todaySeq = 0;

function showToday() {
  for (const el of document.querySelectorAll('[data-today-count]')) {
    el.textContent = String(todayDue);
    el.hidden = todayDue === 0;
  }
  for (const a of menuLinks.today) a.setAttribute('aria-label', todayLinkLabel(todayDue));
}

async function refreshToday() {
  const mine = ++todaySeq;
  let count;
  try {
    count = todayCount(await request('GET', '/api/tasks/today'));
  } catch (err) {
    count = err.status === 404 ? 0 : null;
  }
  if (mine !== todaySeq || count === null) return; // a newer answer wins; no answer keeps the last number
  todayDue = count;
  showToday();
}

live.on('tasks', (data) => {
  todaySeq++; // a read still on its way is older than this
  if (Number.isInteger(data.due)) todayDue = data.due;
  showToday();
});
```
