  if (up) { refreshBadge(); refreshToday(); } // events were missed while it was down
});

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `web/task-settings.js`**

```js
// The Settings page's Workers and Task rules cards (spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md 7.3). Workers: the names picked on Done
// and Skip (add, rename, remove; a removed name stays on the tasks it closed). Task rules: every built-in rule with what it
// applies to and an On/Off switch, the upkeep rules' intervals, and the owner's own rules (Add your own). Each change is
// saved at once (PUT /api/workers, PUT /api/task-rules, with the whole list). mountTaskSettings(container) → unmount().

import { request, OFFLINE, OUTDATED, routeMissing } from './api.js';
import { ruleLine, customLine, SLOT_LABELS } from './tasks-math.js';
import { parseWorkerName, workersAfter, parseEveryDays, parseCustomRule, WORKER_MAX } from './settings-math.js';
import { keepFocus } from './task-list.js';

const COMMAND_TIMEOUT = 15000;
const SLOT_OPTIONS = Object.entries(SLOT_LABELS).map(([value, label]) => `<option value="${value}">${label}</option>`).join('');

const TEMPLATE = `
  <section class="card settings tk-workers" data-workers-card hidden>
    <h2>Workers</h2>
    <p class="muted s-hint">The names to pick from on Done and Skip, on a room's Tasks tab and the Today page. A removed name stays on the tasks it closed.</p>
    <ul class="tk-workers-list" data-workers></ul>
    <form class="tk-add" data-add-worker novalidate>
      <div class="field">
        <label for="f-worker-new">Add a name</label>
        <input id="f-worker-new" name="worker" type="text" autocomplete="off" maxlength="${WORKER_MAX}" aria-describedby="e-worker-new">
        <p class="field-error" id="e-worker-new" role="status"></p>
      </div>
      <button type="submit">Add</button>
    </form>
    <p class="field-error" data-workers-error role="status"></p>
  </section>
  <section class="card settings tk-rules" data-rules-card hidden>
    <h2>Task rules</h2>
    <p class="muted s-hint">Stage tasks come each day from a room's crops and stage; upkeep is counted from the last time it was done. A rule turned off makes no new tasks; its open ones stay until done or the day ends.</p>
    <h3>Stage tasks</h3>
    <ul class="tk-rule-list" data-daily></ul>
    <h3>Upkeep</h3>
    <ul class="tk-rule-list" data-upkeep></ul>
    <h3>Your own</h3>
    <ul class="tk-rule-list" data-custom></ul>
    <p class="muted" data-custom-none>None yet.</p>
    <button type="button" data-add-rule>Add your own</button>
    <form class="tk-rule-form" data-rule-form novalidate hidden>
      <div class="field">
        <label for="f-rule-text">Task</label>
        <input id="f-rule-text" name="text" type="text" autocomplete="off" maxlength="120" aria-describedby="e-rule-text">
        <p class="field-error" id="e-rule-text" role="status"></p>
      </div>
      <fieldset class="tk-rooms" aria-describedby="e-rule-rooms">
        <legend>Rooms</legend>
        <label class="tg-check"><input type="checkbox" name="allRooms" checked> All rooms</label>
        <div data-room-boxes></div>
        <p class="field-error" id="e-rule-rooms" role="status"></p>
      </fieldset>
      <div class="field">
        <label for="f-rule-often">How often</label>
        <select id="f-rule-often" name="often"><option value="daily">Every day</option><option value="every">Every so many days, from the last time done</option></select>
      </div>
      <div class="field" data-every-field hidden>
        <label for="f-rule-every">Every (days)</label>
        <input id="f-rule-every" name="everyDays" type="text" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="off" aria-describedby="e-rule-every">
        <p class="field-error" id="e-rule-every" role="status"></p>
      </div>
      <div class="field">
        <label for="f-rule-slot">When in the day</label>
        <select id="f-rule-slot" name="slot">${SLOT_OPTIONS}</select>
      </div>
      <div class="ac-send">
        <button type="submit" class="primary">Add the rule</button>
        <button type="button" data-rule-cancel>Cancel</button>
      </div>
    </form>
    <p class="s-saved" data-rules-saved role="status" hidden></p>
    <p class="field-error" data-rules-error role="status"></p>
  </section>`;

function el(tag, className, content) {
  const node = document.createElement(tag);
  if (className) node.className = className;
  if (content !== undefined) node.textContent = content;
  return node;
}

function button(label, className, type = 'button') {
  const b = el('button', className ?? '', label);
  b.type = type;
  return b;
}

const errorText = (err) => (err.status === 0 ? `${OFFLINE}. Try again.` : routeMissing(err) ? OUTDATED : err.message);

export function mountTaskSettings(container) {
  let alive = true;
  let busy = false; // one save at a time
  let workers = []; // GET /api/workers' list, the removed ones too
  let rules = null; // GET /api/task-rules
  let rooms = []; // GET /api/rooms, for the own rules' room names
  container.innerHTML = TEMPLATE;
  const q = (sel) => container.querySelector(sel);
  const w = {
    card: q('[data-workers-card]'), list: q('[data-workers]'), form: q('[data-add-worker]'), input: q('#f-worker-new'), err: q('#e-worker-new'),
    error: q('[data-workers-error]'),
  };
  const r = {
    card: q('[data-rules-card]'), daily: q('[data-daily]'), upkeep: q('[data-upkeep]'), custom: q('[data-custom]'), none: q('[data-custom-none]'),
    add: q('[data-add-rule]'), form: q('[data-rule-form]'), boxes: q('[data-room-boxes]'), everyField: q('[data-every-field]'),
    saved: q('[data-rules-saved]'), error: q('[data-rules-error]'),
  };
  const f = {
    text: q('#f-rule-text'), all: q('input[name="allRooms"]'), often: q('#f-rule-often'), every: q('#f-rule-every'), slot: q('#f-rule-slot'),
    errors: { text: q('#e-rule-text'), rooms: q('#e-rule-rooms'), everyDays: q('#e-rule-every') },
  };

  // ---- Workers ----

  function paintWorkers() {
    const active = workers.filter((x) => x.active);
    w.list.replaceChildren(...(active.length ? active.map(workerItem) : [el('li', 'muted', 'No names yet. Add the people who do the rounds.')]));
  }

  async function putWorkers(list, onField) {
    if (busy) return false;
    busy = true;
    w.error.textContent = '';
    try {
      const data = await request('PUT', '/api/workers', { workers: list }, COMMAND_TIMEOUT);
      if (!alive) return false;
      workers = Array.isArray(data?.workers) ? data.workers : list;
      paintWorkers();
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      if (!alive) return false;
      if (err.status === 400 && onField) onField(err.message);
      else w.error.textContent = errorText(err);
      return false;
    } finally {
      busy = false;
    }
  }

  function workerItem(x) {
    const li = el('li');
    const rename = button('Rename');
    rename.setAttribute('aria-label', `Rename ${x.name}`);
    const remove = button('Remove', 'danger-text');
    remove.setAttribute('aria-label', `Remove ${x.name}`);
    rename.addEventListener('click', () => li.replaceWith(renameItem(x)));
    remove.addEventListener('click', () => putWorkers(workersAfter(workers, { remove: x.id })));
    li.append(el('span', 'tk-worker-name', x.name), rename, remove);
    return li;
  }

  function renameItem(x) {
    const li = el('li');
    const form = el('form', 'tk-rename');
    form.noValidate = true;
    const id = `f-worker-${x.id}`;
    const field = el('div', 'field');
    const label = el('label', '', `New name for ${x.name}`);
    label.htmlFor = id;
    const input = el('input');
    input.id = id;
    input.type = 'text';
    input.autocomplete = 'off';
    input.maxLength = WORKER_MAX;
    input.value = x.name;
    const err = el('p', 'field-error');
    err.id = `${id}-error`;
    err.setAttribute('role', 'status');
    input.setAttribute('aria-describedby', err.id);
    field.append(label, input, err);
    const cancel = button('Cancel');
    cancel.addEventListener('click', () => paintWorkers());
    const onField = (text) => { err.textContent = text; input.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true'); input.focus(); };
    form.addEventListener('submit', async (event) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      const p = parseWorkerName(input.value, workers, x.id);
      if (p.error) return onField(p.error);
      await putWorkers(workersAfter(workers, { rename: { id: x.id, name: p.value } }), onField);
    });
    form.append(field, button('Save', 'primary', 'submit'), cancel);
    li.append(form);
    queueMicrotask(() => input.focus());
    return li;
  }

  w.form.addEventListener('submit', async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    w.err.textContent = '';
    w.input.removeAttribute('aria-invalid');
    const onField = (text) => { w.err.textContent = text; w.input.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true'); w.input.focus(); };
    const p = parseWorkerName(w.input.value, workers);
    if (p.error) return onField(p.error);
    if (await putWorkers(workersAfter(workers, { add: p.value }), onField)) {
      w.input.value = '';
      w.input.focus();
    }
  });

  // ---- Task rules ----

  const withBuiltin = (key, change) => ({ ...rules, builtin: rules.builtin.map((b) => (b.key === key ? { ...b, ...change } : b)) });
  const withCustom = (id, change) => ({ ...rules, custom: rules.custom.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, ...change } : c)) });

  // On/Off, as the machines' switches (DESIGN.md: On tinted, never filled).
  function toggle(on, name, key, onClick) {
    const b = button('', 'toggle');
    b.dataset.on = String(on);
    b.dataset.focusKey = key;
    b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(on));
    b.append(el('span', 'sr-only', `${name}: `), el('span', '', on ? 'On' : 'Off'));
    b.addEventListener('click', onClick);
    return b;
  }

  function ruleItem(rule, line, key, onToggle, extra = []) {
    const li = el('li');
    const text = el('div', 'tk-rule-text');
    text.append(el('p', '', rule.text));
    if (line) text.append(el('p', 'tk-rule-line', line));
    li.append(text, ...extra, toggle(rule.enabled, rule.text, key, onToggle));
    return li;
  }

  function everyBox(rule) {
    const label = el('label', 'tk-every');
    const input = el('input');
    input.type = 'text';
    input.inputMode = 'numeric';
    input.autocomplete = 'off';
    input.value = String(rule.everyDays);
    input.dataset.focusKey = `every-${rule.key}`;
    input.setAttribute('aria-label', `${rule.text}: every how many days`);
    label.append(el('span', '', 'Every'), input, el('span', '', 'days'));
    input.addEventListener('change', () => {
      const p = parseEveryDays(input.value);
      if (p.error) {
        r.error.textContent = `${rule.text}: ${p.error}`;
        input.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
        return;
      }
      input.removeAttribute('aria-invalid');
      if (p.value !== rule.everyDays) putRules(withBuiltin(rule.key, { everyDays: p.value }));
    });
    return label;
  }

  function paintRules() {
    keepFocus(r.card, () => {
      r.daily.replaceChildren(...rules.builtin.filter((b) => b.kind === 'daily')
        .map((b) => ruleItem(b, ruleLine(b), `rule-${b.key}`, () => putRules(withBuiltin(b.key, { enabled: !b.enabled })))));
      r.upkeep.replaceChildren(...rules.builtin.filter((b) => b.kind === 'upkeep')
        .map((b) => ruleItem(b, '', `rule-${b.key}`, () => putRules(withBuiltin(b.key, { enabled: !b.enabled })), [everyBox(b)])));
      r.custom.replaceChildren(...rules.custom
        .map((c) => ruleItem(c, customLine(c, rooms), `custom-${c.id}`, () => putRules(withCustom(c.id, { enabled: !c.enabled })))));
      r.none.hidden = rules.custom.length > 0;
    });
  }

  async function putRules(next) {
    if (busy) return false;
    busy = true;
    r.error.textContent = '';
    r.saved.hidden = true;
    const body = {
      builtin: next.builtin.map((b) => ({ key: b.key, enabled: b.enabled, ...(b.kind === 'upkeep' ? { everyDays: b.everyDays } : {}) })),
      custom: next.custom,
    };
    try {
      const data = await request('PUT', '/api/task-rules', body, COMMAND_TIMEOUT);
      if (!alive) return false;
      rules = data;
      r.saved.textContent = 'Saved';
      r.saved.hidden = false;
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      if (!alive) return false;
      r.error.textContent = errorText(err);
      return false;
    } finally {
      busy = false;
      if (alive && rules) paintRules(); // the saved rules, or (after a failure) the ones before
    }
  }

  function paintForm() {
    r.everyField.hidden = f.often.value !== 'every';
    r.boxes.hidden = f.all.checked;
  }
  function clearFormErrors() {
    for (const node of Object.values(f.errors)) node.textContent = '';
    for (const input of [f.text, f.every, f.all]) input.removeAttribute('aria-invalid');
  }
  f.often.addEventListener('change', paintForm);
  f.all.addEventListener('change', paintForm);

  r.add.addEventListener('click', () => {
    f.text.value = '';
    f.all.checked = true;
    f.often.value = 'daily';
    f.every.value = '';
    f.slot.value = 'morning';
    clearFormErrors();
    r.boxes.replaceChildren(...rooms.map((room) => {
      const label = el('label', 'tg-check');
      const box = el('input');
      box.type = 'checkbox';
      box.name = 'rooms';
      box.value = String(room.id);
      label.append(box, ` ${room.name}`);
      return label;
    }));
    paintForm();
    r.form.hidden = false;
    r.add.hidden = true;
    f.text.focus();
  });

  q('[data-rule-cancel]').addEventListener('click', () => {
    r.form.hidden = true;
    r.add.hidden = false;
    r.add.focus();
  });

  r.form.addEventListener('submit', async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    clearFormErrors();
    if (!rules) return;
    const p = parseCustomRule({
      text: f.text.value, allRooms: f.all.checked, rooms: [...r.boxes.querySelectorAll('input[name="rooms"]:checked')].map((b) => Number(b.value)),
      often: f.often.value, everyDays: f.every.value, slot: f.slot.value,
    });
    if (p.errors) {
      const names = Object.keys(p.errors);
      for (const name of names) {
        if (f.errors[name]) f.errors[name].textContent = p.errors[name];
        else r.error.textContent = p.errors[name];
      }
      const first = { text: f.text, rooms: f.all, everyDays: f.every }[names[0]];
      first?.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
      first?.focus();
      return;
    }
    if (await putRules({ ...rules, custom: [...rules.custom, p.value] })) {
      r.form.hidden = true;
      r.add.hidden = false;
      r.add.focus();
    }
  });

  // ---- loading ----

  async function load() {
    const [wk, rl, rm] = await Promise.allSettled([request('GET', '/api/workers'), request('GET', '/api/task-rules'), request('GET', '/api/rooms')]);
    if (!alive) return;
    if (rm.status === 'fulfilled' && Array.isArray(rm.value)) rooms = rm.value;
    // A farm app from before the tasks has neither route: the two cards stay hidden.
    if (wk.status === 'fulfilled') {
      workers = Array.isArray(wk.value?.workers) ? wk.value.workers : [];
      paintWorkers();
      w.card.hidden = false;
    } else if (!routeMissing(wk.reason)) {
      w.error.textContent = `Couldn't load the names: ${errorText(wk.reason)}`;
      w.card.hidden = false;
    }
    if (rl.status === 'fulfilled' && rl.value) {
      rules = rl.value;
      paintRules();
      r.card.hidden = false;
    } else if (rl.status === 'rejected' && !routeMissing(rl.reason)) {
      r.error.textContent = `Couldn't load the rules: ${errorText(rl.reason)}`;
      r.add.hidden = true;
      r.card.hidden = false;
    }
  }

  load();
  return function unmount() {
    alive = false;
    container.replaceChildren();
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Append to `web/styles.css`**

```css

/* ---- Tasks (iteration 19, spec 2026-10-30-tasks-design.md 7): the room's Tasks tab, the Today page, the task dialog,
   the Settings cards and Plan this. Tokens only; amber for overdue, never red (a task is not an alarm). ---- */
.room-tabs .tab-dot.tab-count { width: auto; height: auto; min-width: 18px; padding: 0 5px; border-radius: 9px; background: var(--band); color: var(--ink-2);
  font: 700 11px/18px var(--font-body); text-align: center; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.room-tabs .tab-dot.tab-count[data-level="warning"] { background: var(--warning-soft); color: var(--warning-text); }
.top-link .top-count { display: inline-block; min-width: 18px; padding: 0 5px; border-radius: 9px; background: var(--raised); border: 1px solid var(--border-strong);
  color: var(--ink); font: 700 11px/16px var(--font-body); text-align: center; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }

.tk-grid { display: grid; gap: 16px; align-items: start; }
@media (min-width: 1100px) {
  .tk-grid { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 3fr) minmax(0, 2fr); }
  .tk-grid > .tk-today { grid-row: 1 / span 3; }
}
.tk-part .sim-card-head { flex-wrap: wrap; }
.tk-sub { margin: 0 0 8px; font-size: var(--fs-sm); }
.tk-part .none, .td-room .none { margin: 8px 0 0; color: var(--muted); }
.tk-error { margin: 0 0 8px; }
.tk-list { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
.tk-row { display: flex; align-items: flex-start; justify-content: space-between; gap: 12px; padding: 10px 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); }
.tk-list > .tk-row:first-child { border-top: 0; }
.tk-text { flex: 1 1 auto; min-width: 0; }
.tk-title { margin: 0; font-weight: 500; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.tk-row:not([data-status="open"]) .tk-title { color: var(--ink-2); font-weight: 400; }
.tk-note, .tk-closed-line { margin: 2px 0 0; color: var(--muted); font-size: var(--fs-sm); }
.tk-due { margin: 2px 0 0; color: var(--ink-2); font-size: var(--fs-sm); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.tk-due[data-tone="amber"] { color: var(--warning-text); font-weight: 600; }
.tk-actions { display: flex; gap: 8px; flex: none; }
.tk-slot + .tk-slot { margin-top: 12px; }
.tk-slot-title { margin: 0 0 4px; font-size: var(--fs-sm); font-weight: 600; color: var(--ink-2); }
.tk-fold { margin-top: 4px; }
.tk-fold > summary { cursor: pointer; padding: 6px 0; color: var(--muted); font-size: var(--fs-sm); }
.tk-as { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; font-size: var(--fs-sm); color: var(--ink-2); }
.tk-as > span { white-space: nowrap; }
.tk-as select { font: inherit; min-height: 36px; padding: 6px 8px; border: 1px solid var(--border-strong); border-radius: var(--radius); background: var(--surface); color: var(--ink); }
.tk-log { list-style: none; margin: 0 0 8px; padding: 0; }
.tk-log li { padding: 8px 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); }
.tk-log li:first-child { border-top: 0; }
.tk-log-head { margin: 0; color: var(--ink-2); font-size: var(--fs-sm); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.tk-log-status[data-tone="good"] { color: var(--good-text); font-weight: 600; }
.tk-log-status[data-tone="amber"] { color: var(--warning-text); font-weight: 600; }
.tk-log-task { margin: 2px 0 0; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.tk-log-reason { margin: 2px 0 0; color: var(--muted); font-size: var(--fs-sm); }

.td-picker { flex: none; }
.td-rooms { display: grid; gap: 16px; align-items: start; }
@media (min-width: 1100px) { .td-rooms { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); } }
.td-room h2 a { color: var(--ink); text-decoration: none; }
.td-room h2 a:hover { text-decoration: underline; }
.td-long { margin-top: 16px; }
.td-long-list { margin: 0; padding-left: 18px; }
.td-long-list li { padding: 4px 0; }

.tk-dialog .tk-lead { margin: 0 0 12px; color: var(--ink-2); }
.tk-dialog .tk-changes { margin: 0 0 12px; padding-left: 18px; }
.tk-dialog textarea { font: inherit; width: 100%; padding: 8px 10px; border: 1px solid var(--border-strong); border-radius: var(--radius); background: var(--surface); color: var(--ink); resize: vertical; }
.tk-dialog .tk-check { flex-direction: row; align-items: center; gap: 8px; }
.tk-dialog .tk-check input { width: 20px; height: 20px; }
.tk-dialog .actions { flex-wrap: wrap; }

.tk-settings { display: contents; }
.tk-workers-list, .tk-rule-list { list-style: none; margin: 0 0 12px; padding: 0; }
.tk-workers-list li, .tk-rule-list li { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; gap: 8px 12px; padding: 8px 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); }
.tk-workers-list li:first-child, .tk-rule-list li:first-child { border-top: 0; }
.tk-worker-name { flex: 1 1 auto; min-width: 0; font-weight: 500; }
.tk-rename { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: end; gap: 8px; width: 100%; }
.tk-rename .field { flex: 1 1 200px; margin: 0; }
.tk-add { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: end; gap: 8px; }
.tk-add .field { margin: 0; }
.tk-rules h3 { margin: 16px 0 4px; font-size: var(--fs-md); font-weight: 600; }
.tk-rule-text { flex: 1 1 240px; min-width: 0; }
.tk-rule-text p { margin: 0; }
.tk-rule-line { color: var(--muted); font-size: var(--fs-sm); }
.tk-every { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; font-size: var(--fs-sm); color: var(--ink-2); }
.tk-every input { width: 64px; font: inherit; min-height: 36px; padding: 6px 8px; border: 1px solid var(--border-strong); border-radius: var(--radius); background: var(--surface); color: var(--ink); }
.tk-every input[aria-invalid="true"] { border-color: var(--critical-text); }
.tk-rule-form { margin-top: 12px; }
.tk-rooms { border: 0; margin: 0 0 12px; padding: 0; }
.tk-rooms legend { padding: 0; margin-bottom: 4px; font-size: 0.9rem; font-weight: 600; }

.sg-use { display: grid; gap: 6px; }
.sg-planned { margin: 4px 0 0; font-size: var(--fs-sm); }

@media (max-width: 600px) {
  .tk-row { flex-direction: column; align-items: stretch; gap: 8px; }
  .tk-actions button { flex: 1 1 0; min-height: 44px; }
  .tk-fold > summary { min-height: 44px; padding: 12px 0; }
  .tk-as { width: 100%; }
  .tk-as select { flex: 1 1 auto; min-height: 44px; }
  .td-picker { width: 100%; }
  .tk-every input { min-height: 44px; }
  .sg-use { grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; }
  .top-link { min-width: 44px; padding: 4px 6px; justify-content: center; } /* room for the Today link beside the farm's name */
}
```

### Task C12: README

**Files:**
- Modify: `README.md` (insert a paragraph after line 53, before line 55 "The farm app polls …")

- [ ] **Step 1: Insert after line 53 (with a blank line before and after)**

```markdown
**Tasks.** Each room has a **Tasks** tab (between Crop and Simulations, with the number of tasks due on it), and the top bar a **Today** link with the farm's count. Daily stage tasks come from the room's crops, stage and day in the stage: the contamination walk every morning, the colonisation check in the last week of the spawn run, opening the bags (or the casing soil for milky) on the first day of pinning, the humidifier check, the harvest checks while fruiting (morning and afternoon for oyster and lion's mane; the crop's notes under them, such as "pick on sale days" for pink oyster), scrape and recap at the start of a rest and reopen at its end, the footbath and the floor every day; a room without crops gets only the footbath and the floor, and after the last flush "clear out the spent blocks". A day's tasks still open when it ends (Asia/Kuala_Lumpur) count as missed; a stage change cancels the ones that no longer apply. Upkeep repeats from the last time it was done (the aircon filter, the humidifier and the fly traps every 7 days, the dehumidifier's filter every 14, the CO₂ sensor every 30 and the RH sensor every 90, the aircon service every 120 and its chemical wash every 180, each only when the room has that machine or sensor); one past its date says "overdue 3 days". Long-term tasks come from **Plan this** on Suggest machines (a task per machine that differs from what the room has now, with the RM a month against it) or **Add** on the tab; **Done** on a machine task offers to update the room's Setup (the aircon's rated input from its BTU/h ÷ 11.25, the humidifier's output, the dehumidifier) and marks it done only once Setup is saved. Every Done and Skip records who (a name from Settings → Workers; "Doing the rounds as" keeps it in the browser), when and, for the harvest checks, the kg picked; a skip or a cancel needs a reason. The Log shows the last 14 days (**Older…** the rest) and **Export CSV** gives every closed task for the myGAP records. Settings → Task rules turns each built-in rule off or on, sets the upkeep intervals (1–365 days) and adds your own. Spec: `docs/specs/2026-10-30-tasks-design.md`.
```

### Task C13: live check (after Parts A and B are merged into the worktree)

No repo file changes: nothing to commit. The check runs a **test copy** of the farm app on spare ports, on a copy of the owner's database whose rooms' gateways (and the farm gateway) point at a dead port, with Telegram cleared (the copy's rooms go offline and would raise alarms). **Never** the live gateways `127.0.0.1:1512`–`1514`, never `data/v2.db` itself, never port 8080.

**Files:**
- Create (outside the repo): `/home/foqcus/.claude/task-logs/tasks-tab/shot.mjs` (a full-page screenshot through the Chrome DevTools Protocol, once the page says it has its data; plain `chrome --headless --screenshot` hangs or shoots too early on these pages because of the live stream)
- Create (outside the repo): `/home/foqcus/.claude/task-logs/tasks-tab/live-check.sh`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the screenshot helper** `/home/foqcus/.claude/task-logs/tasks-tab/shot.mjs` (tried 2026-10-30 against a scratch copy of the app with Part C applied)

```js
// A full-page screenshot through the Chrome DevTools Protocol, once the page says it is ready.
//   node shot.mjs <url> <width> <out.png> [ready: a JS expression, true when the page has painted its data]
//                 [action: a JS expression run once ready, such as a click that opens a dialog; the shot is then the viewport]
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import fs from 'node:fs';

const [url, width, out, ready = 'true', action = ''] = process.argv.slice(2);
const port = 9300 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 600);
const chrome = spawn('google-chrome-stable', ['--headless=new', '--disable-gpu', '--no-first-run', '--hide-scrollbars',
  `--remote-debugging-port=${port}`, `--user-data-dir=/tmp/tasks-check-chrome-${port}`, 'about:blank'], { stdio: 'ignore' });
const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
try {
  let target;
  for (let i = 0; i < 50 && !target; i++) {
    await sleep(200);
    try { target = (await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/json`)).json()).find((t) => t.type === 'page'); } catch { /* not up yet */ }
  }
  const ws = new WebSocket(target.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
  await new Promise((r) => ws.addEventListener('open', r, { once: true }));
  let seq = 0;
  const pending = new Map();
  ws.addEventListener('message', (e) => { const m = JSON.parse(e.data); if (m.id && pending.has(m.id)) { pending.get(m.id)(m); pending.delete(m.id); } });
  const send = (method, params = {}) => new Promise((r) => { const id = ++seq; pending.set(id, r); ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id, method, params })); });
  const w = Number(width);
  await send('Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride', { width: w, height: 900, deviceScaleFactor: 1, mobile: w < 600 });
  await send('Page.enable');
  await send('Page.navigate', { url });
  let ok = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < 80 && !ok; i++) {
    await sleep(250);
    const r = await send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: `(() => { try { return Boolean(${ready}); } catch { return false; } })()`, returnByValue: true });
    ok = r.result?.result?.value === true;
  }
  await sleep(400);
  if (action) {
    await send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: action });
    await sleep(800);
  }
  const { result: m } = await send('Page.getLayoutMetrics');
  const h = action ? 900 : Math.ceil(m.cssContentSize.height);
  await send('Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride', { width: w, height: h, deviceScaleFactor: 1, mobile: w < 600 });
  await sleep(300);
  const shot = await send('Page.captureScreenshot', { format: 'png' });
  fs.writeFileSync(out, Buffer.from(shot.result.data, 'base64'));
  console.log(`${out}: ${w} × ${h}${ok ? '' : ' (NOT READY: the page never said it was)'}`);
  ws.close();
} finally {
  const gone = new Promise((r) => chrome.once('exit', r));
  chrome.kill();
  await gone;
  try { fs.rmSync(`/tmp/tasks-check-chrome-${port}`, { recursive: true, force: true, maxRetries: 5, retryDelay: 200 }); } catch { /* Chrome's helpers still closing: /tmp keeps it */ }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the script**

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Live check of the Tasks tab and Today (plan part C, task C13). A TEST copy of the farm app: a snapshot of the owner's
# database, every room's gateway and the farm gateway on a dead port, Telegram cleared, on port 18080. Never the live
# gateways 127.0.0.1:1512-1514, never data/v2.db itself, never port 8080.
set -euo pipefail
WT=/home/foqcus/Documents/mushroom-farm/.claude/worktrees/tasks
LIVE_DB=/home/foqcus/Documents/mushroom-farm/data/v2.db
DIR=/tmp/tasks-check
PORT=18080
DEAD=127.0.0.1:1599
ROOM=${ROOM:-2}

ss -ltn | grep -q ":1599 " && { echo "port 1599 answers: not a dead port"; exit 1; }
ss -ltn | grep -q ":$PORT " && { echo "port $PORT is in use"; exit 1; }
rm -rf "$DIR"; mkdir -p "$DIR"

# 1. a consistent snapshot of the live database (read-only on the live file)
sqlite3 -readonly "$LIVE_DB" ".backup '$DIR/v2.db'"
# 2. every room on the dead gateway; no Telegram from the copy
sqlite3 "$DIR/v2.db" "UPDATE rooms SET gateway = '$DEAD'; DELETE FROM settings WHERE key LIKE 'telegram%';"
LEFT=$(sqlite3 "$DIR/v2.db" "SELECT count(*) FROM rooms WHERE gateway IS NULL OR gateway <> '$DEAD';")
[ "$LEFT" = 0 ] || { echo "a room still points elsewhere"; exit 1; }
sqlite3 -header "$DIR/v2.db" "SELECT id, name, gateway FROM rooms;"
sqlite3 "$DIR/v2.db" "SELECT id FROM rooms WHERE id = $ROOM;" | grep -q . || { echo "no room $ROOM in the copy: run with ROOM=<id>"; exit 1; }

# 3. the worktree's farm app on the copy (migrates the copy 18 → 19)
cd "$WT"
FARM_PORT=$PORT FARM_DB="$DIR/v2.db" FARM_GATEWAY=$DEAD node --disable-warning=ExperimentalWarning server/main.js > "$DIR/farm.log" 2>&1 &
APP=$!
trap 'kill $APP 2>/dev/null || true' EXIT
for _ in $(seq 1 50); do curl -sf "http://127.0.0.1:$PORT/api/rooms" > /dev/null && break; sleep 0.2; done
sqlite3 "$DIR/v2.db" "PRAGMA user_version;"   # expect 19

# 4. the API on the copy: workers, the room's tasks, Today, one Done, a second Done (409), the CSV
B="http://127.0.0.1:$PORT"
curl -sf -X PUT -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"workers":[{"name":"Ali","active":true},{"name":"Siti","active":true}]}' "$B/api/workers"; echo
curl -sf "$B/api/rooms/$ROOM/tasks" > "$DIR/room-tasks.json"
curl -sf "$B/api/tasks/today" > "$DIR/today.json"
node -e 'const v=JSON.parse(require("fs").readFileSync(process.argv[1],"utf8"));console.log("date",v.date,"counts",JSON.stringify(v.counts),"today",v.today.length,"upkeep",v.upkeep.length,"longterm",v.longterm.length)' "$DIR/room-tasks.json"
FIRST=$(node -e 'const v=JSON.parse(require("fs").readFileSync(process.argv[1],"utf8"));const r=v.today.find((t)=>t.status==="open"&&!t.unit&&!t.amountLabel);console.log(r?r.id:"")' "$DIR/room-tasks.json")
if [ -n "$FIRST" ]; then
  curl -sf -X POST -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"worker":"Ali"}' "$B/api/tasks/$FIRST/done"; echo
  curl -s -o /dev/null -w 'second Done: %{http_code} (expect 409)\n' -X POST -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"worker":"Ali"}' "$B/api/tasks/$FIRST/done"
fi
curl -sf "$B/api/rooms/$ROOM/tasks/log.csv" | head -3

# 5. screenshots, dark (the default theme), at 390 px and 1440 px, once each page shows its data; then the Done dialog of
#    a row that records an amount (the harvest check), at 390 px
SHOT=/home/foqcus/.claude/task-logs/tasks-tab/shot.mjs
TASKS_READY="!document.querySelector('[data-panel=\"tasks\"] .tk-sub')?.textContent.startsWith('Loading')"
TODAY_READY="!document.querySelector('[data-sub]')?.textContent.startsWith('Loading')"
for W in 390 1440; do
  timeout 60 node "$SHOT" "$B/#/rooms/$ROOM/tasks" $W "$DIR/room-$ROOM-tasks-$W.png" "$TASKS_READY"
  timeout 60 node "$SHOT" "$B/#/today" $W "$DIR/today-$W.png" "$TODAY_READY"
done
timeout 60 node "$SHOT" "$B/#/rooms/$ROOM/tasks" 390 "$DIR/room-$ROOM-done-dialog-390.png" "$TASKS_READY" \
  "[...document.querySelectorAll('[data-panel=\"tasks\"] .tk-row')].find((li) => /Harvest/.test(li.textContent) && li.dataset.status === 'open')?.querySelector('button')?.click()"
timeout 60 node "$SHOT" "$B/#/settings" 1440 "$DIR/settings-1440.png" "!document.querySelector('[data-rules-card]')?.hidden"
grep -i "error" "$DIR/farm.log" | head -20 || true
```

## Contract points Part C relies on (for Parts A/B to confirm)

| Point | What Part C does |
|---|---|
| SSE event type `tasks` | Part C adds it to `web/live.js` `EVENT_TYPES` (C4); without it `live.on('tasks')` would throw |
| `PUT /api/workers` body | sends `{ workers: [{ id?, name, active }] }` (the whole list; a removed worker is sent `active: false`, never left out); expects `{ workers }` back |
| `PUT /api/task-rules` body | sends `{ builtin: [{ key, enabled, everyDays? }] (everyDays only for upkeep), custom: [{ id?, text, rooms, everyDays, slot, enabled }] }` (the whole custom list; a new one without `id`; none is ever left out) |
| `GET /api/tasks/today` rooms | uses `counts.due` when present (contract's `todayView`), else counts open `today` + `upkeepDue` rows |
| upkeep rows | uses `dueDate` when present, else the farm day of `dueAt` |
| open rows that record an amount | need `unit` and/or `amountLabel` (contract row) |
| closed rows | `closedAt` set for missed and cancelled too (the Log's time; falls back to `createdAt`) |
| 400 `field` names | `worker`, `reason`, `amount`, `text`, `note` (shown under that box) |
| unknown limits | client checks reason ≤ 200, long-term text ≤ 120, note ≤ 500 characters; the server's own limits should be at least these |
| `POST /api/rooms/:id/tasks` reply | `{ rows: Row[] }` (Plan this counts them) |
